Verse 5.102

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

निशम्येति वचः शुम्भः स तदा चण्डमुण्डयोः।प्रेषयामास सुग्रीवं दूतं देव्या महासुरम्॥⁠१०२⁠॥

niśamyeti vacaḥ śumbhaḥ sa tadā caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥ |preṣayāmāsa sugrīvaṃ dūtaṃ devyā mahāsuram ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

निशम्य · इति · वचः · शुम्भः · सः · तदा · चण्डमुण्डयोःप्रेषयामास · सुग्रीवम् · दूतम् · देव्याः · महासुरम्

niśamya · iti · vacaḥ · śumbhaḥ · saḥ · tadā · caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥpreṣayāmāsa · sugrīvam · dūtam · devyāḥ · mahāsuram

Word by word

  1. निशम्यniśamyahaving heardabsolutive (lyap) of नि + √शम्
  2. इतिitithus, these (words)indeclinable (refers back to the speech just reported)
  3. वचःvacaḥspeech, wordsn. acc. sg. of वचस्; object of निशम्य
  4. शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject of प्रेषयामास
  5. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; resumes शुम्भः
  6. तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  7. चण्डमुण्डयोःcaṇḍamuṇḍayoḥof Caṇḍa and Muṇḍam. gen. du.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on वचः
  8. प्रेषयामासpreṣayāmāsasentcausative of प्र + √इष् (divādi, 4P ‘to send forth’), periphrastic perfect (āmantaliṭ), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  9. सुग्रीवम्sugrīvamSugrīvam. acc. sg.; proper name; object of प्रेषयामास
  10. दूतम्dūtamas messengerm. acc. sg. of दूत; predicative accusative with सुग्रीवम्
  11. देव्याःdevyāḥto the Devīf. gen. sg. of देवी; with दूतम् ‘messenger to the Devī’
  12. महासुरम्mahāsuramthe great asuram. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies सुग्रीवम्

Translation

Having heard these words of Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa, Śumbha then sent the great asura Sugrīva as messenger to the Devī.

In readable EnglishHearing what Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa said, Śumbha sent the mighty demon Sugrīva as his envoy to the Goddess.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • निशम्येति niśamyeti → निशम्य + इति

    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + i → e.

  • वचः शुम्भः स तदा vacaḥ śumbhaḥ sa tadā → वचः + शुम्भः + सः + तदा

    visarga sandhi · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)

    Before the sibilants ś and s the visarga of वचः and शुम्भः is kept (an optional alternative to s/ś, which the Gita Press prefers); the visarga of सः is dropped before the consonant t.

  • सुग्रीवं दूतं देव्या महासुरम् sugrīvaṃ dūtaṃ devyā mahāsuram → सुग्रीवम् + दूतम् + देव्याः + महासुरम्

    anusvāra sandhi; visarga sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; -āḥ before the voiced m loses its visarga.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • चण्डमुण्डयोः caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥ

    itaretara dvandva

    Members
    चण्ड + मुण्ड
    Vigraha
    चण्डः च मुण्डः च, तयोः caṇḍaḥ ca muṇḍaḥ ca, tayoḥ
    Literally
    “of Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa”
    In the verse
    the two servants who made the report

  • सुग्रीवम् sugrīvam

    bahuvrīhi (ग्रीवा shortened to -ग्रीव at the end)

    Members
    सु + ग्रीवा
    Vigraha
    शोभना ग्रीवा यस्य सः, तम् śobhanā grīvā yasya saḥ, tam
    Literally
    “he whose neck is handsome”
    In the verse
    a proper name: the demon messenger (not the monkey-king of the Rāmāyaṇa)

  • महासुरम् mahāsuram

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    महत् + असुर
    Vigraha
    महान् असुरः, तम् mahān asuraḥ, tam
    Literally
    “a great asura”
    In the verse
    a mighty demon
Morphology & anvaya

  • प्रेषयामास preṣayāmāsa

    Periphrastic perfect of the causative stem प्रेषय- (प्र + √इष्, ‘send’): stem + -आम् + the perfect of √अस् (आस). Derived stems form their perfect this way.

  • निशम्य niśamya

    Absolutive with a prefix, so -ya (lyap) instead of -tvā: ‘having heard’. नि + √शम्, as in निशामय (1.2).

  • देव्याः devyāḥ

    Genitive with दूतम्: the person to whom a messenger is sent may be put in the genitive (‘messenger to the Devī’).

Anvaya · prose order

चण्डमुण्डयोः इति वचः निशम्य सः शुम्भः तदा महासुरं सुग्रीवं देव्याः दूतं प्रेषयामास।

caṇḍamuṇḍayoḥ iti vacaḥ niśamya saḥ śumbhaḥ tadā mahāsuraṃ sugrīvaṃ devyāḥ dūtaṃ preṣayāmāsa |

An absolutive clause (निशम्य … वचः), then subject (शुम्भः, resumed by सः), verb प्रेषयामास, and object सुग्रीवम् with the predicate दूतम्.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Śumbha acts at once on the report: he does not go to see her, as the servants urged (5.92), but sends an envoy, the asura Sugrīva. His instructions follow in 5.103.

Traditional interpretation

The embassy gives the chapter its name in the colophon, देव्या दूतसंवादः ‘the Goddess’s dialogue with the messenger’. Sugrīva the demon is a different figure from the monkey-king of the Rāmāyaṇa who bears the same name.

Verse 5.103

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

इति चेति च वक्तव्या सा गत्वा वचनान्मम।यथा चाभ्येति सम्प्रीत्या तथा कार्यं त्वया लघु॥⁠१०३⁠॥

iti ceti ca vaktavyā sā gatvā vacanānmama |yathā cābhyeti samprītyā tathā kāryaṃ tvayā laghu ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

इति · च · इति · च · वक्तव्या · सा · गत्वा · वचनात् · ममयथा · च · अभ्येति · सम्प्रीत्या · तथा · कार्यम् · त्वया · लघु

iti · ca · iti · ca · vaktavyā · sā · gatvā · vacanāt · mamayathā · ca · abhyeti · samprītyā · tathā · kāryam · tvayā · laghu

Word by word

  1. इतिitithusindeclinable (quotation marker)
  2. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  3. इतिitithusindeclinable (quotation marker)
  4. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); इति च इति च = ‘thus and thus, such and such’
  5. वक्तव्याvaktavyāis to be toldgerundive (tavya) of √वच् (adādi, 2P), f. nom. sg.; predicate of सा
  6. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of the passive वक्तव्या
  7. गत्वाgatvāhaving goneabsolutive (ktvā) of √गम् (bhvādi, 1P)
  8. वचनात्vacanāton the word, by the commandn. abl. sg. of वचन; ablative of authority
  9. ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; qualifies वचनात्
  10. यथाyathāso that, in such a way thatindeclinable (relative adverb; correlative of तथा)
  11. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  12. अभ्येतिabhyeticomes (to me)अभि + √इ (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  13. सम्प्रीत्याsamprītyāwith good will, gladlyf. instr. sg. of सम्प्रीति; instrumental of manner
  14. तथाtathāso, thusindeclinable (correlative of यथा)
  15. कार्यम्kāryam(it) is to be donegerundive (ṇyat) of √कृ, n. nom. sg., impersonal
  16. त्वयाtvayāby yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of कार्यम्
  17. लघुlaghuquicklyn. acc. sg. of लघु, used adverbially

Translation

Having gone, she is to be told on my word such-and-such; and so that she comes with good will, so is it to be done by you, quickly.

In readable English‘Go to her and tell her this and this from me; and act quickly, so that she comes to me willingly.’

The gerundives are rendered as imperatives addressed to the messenger, which is their force.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • इति चेति च iti ceti ca → इति + च + इति + च

    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + i → e (च + इति → चेति).

  • वचनान्मम vacanānmama → वचनात् + मम

    consonant sandhi (nasal assimilation) · यरोऽनुनासिकेऽनुनासिको वा (8.4.45)

    Final t before the nasal m becomes n.

  • चाभ्येति cābhyeti → च + अभ्येति

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā. Inside the verb, अभि + एति → अभ्येति by yaṇ (6.1.77).

  • कार्यं त्वया kāryaṃ tvayā → कार्यम् + त्वया

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

No compound in this verse needs analysis.

Morphology & anvaya

  • वक्तव्या vaktavyā

    Gerundive of √वच् (वच् + tavya, with c → k): ‘she is to be told’. The person told is the grammatical subject, as with verbs of speaking in the passive.

  • गत्वा gatvā

    Absolutive of √गम् (the m drops before -tvā). Its doer is the messenger, who is also the logical agent of वक्तव्या and of कार्यम् (त्वया).

  • अभ्येति abhyeti

    Present of √इ ‘go’ (एति) with the prefix अभि ‘towards’: ‘comes to (me)’. Present tense after यथा for a purpose: ‘so that she comes’.

  • कार्यम् kāryam

    Gerundive of √कृ with the affix ṇyat and vṛddhi (कार्-य): ‘to be done’, used impersonally with the agent त्वया.

Anvaya · prose order

(त्वया) गत्वा मम वचनात् सा ‘इति च इति च’ वक्तव्या; यथा च (सा) सम्प्रीत्या अभ्येति, तथा त्वया लघु कार्यम्।

(tvayā) gatvā mama vacanāt sā ‘iti ca iti ca’ vaktavyā; yathā ca (sā) samprītyā abhyeti, tathā tvayā laghu kāryam |

Śumbha’s instructions in two passive sentences built on gerundives: वक्तव्या (‘she is to be told’) and कार्यम् (‘it is to be done’), the second with a यथा … तथा clause of purpose.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Śumbha’s instructions to Sugrīva, given without a speaker label: speak to her on my authority, and bring her quickly and willingly. इति च इति च stands for the message itself, which the envoy delivers in full in 5.106–114.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: Śumbha wants her consent (सम्प्रीत्या, ‘gladly’) but only as a means — ‘arrange it so that she comes’. Courtesy put to the service of possession is still possession.

Verse 5.104

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

स तत्र गत्वा यत्रास्ते शैलोद्देशेऽतिशोभने।सा देवी तां ततः प्राह श्लक्ष्णं मधुरया गिरा॥⁠१०४⁠॥

sa tatra gatvā yatrāste śailoddeśe'tiśobhane |sā devī tāṃ tataḥ prāha ślakṣṇaṃ madhurayā girā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सः · तत्र · गत्वा · यत्र · आस्ते · शैलोद्देशे · अतिशोभनेसा · देवी · ताम् · ततः · प्राह · श्लक्ष्णम् · मधुरया · गिरा

saḥ · tatra · gatvā · yatra · āste · śailoddeśe · atiśobhanesā · devī · tām · tataḥ · prāha · ślakṣṇam · madhurayā · girā

Word by word

  1. सःsaḥhe (Sugrīva)pronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of प्राह
  2. तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place; correlative of यत्र)
  3. गत्वाgatvāhaving goneabsolutive (ktvā) of √गम्
  4. यत्रyatrawhereindeclinable (relative adverb of place)
  5. आस्तेāstewas staying√आस् (adādi, 2Ā), laṭ (present, here with past sense), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
  6. शैलोद्देशेśailoddeśein a region of the mountainm. loc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  7. अतिशोभनेatiśobhanemost beautifulm. loc. sg. of अतिशोभन; qualifies शैलोद्देशे
  8. साsāthatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies देवी
  9. देवीdevīthe Devīf. nom. sg. of देवी; subject of आस्ते (in the kept reading)
  10. ताम्tāmto herpronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; the person addressed by प्राह
  11. ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  12. प्राहprāhaspokeप्र + √अह् (defective root ‘to say’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  13. श्लक्ष्णम्ślakṣṇamgently, smoothlyn. acc. sg. of श्लक्ष्ण, used adverbially
  14. मधुरयाmadhurayāwith sweetf. instr. sg. of मधुर; qualifies गिरा
  15. गिराgirāspeech, voicef. instr. sg. of गिर् (consonant stem); instrumental of means

Translation

He, having gone there where that Devī was staying, on a most beautiful region of the mountain, then spoke to her gently with sweet speech.

In readable EnglishSugrīva went to the lovely mountain slope where the Goddess was staying, and addressed her gently, in honeyed words.

The kept reading places सा देवी, the subject of the relative clause, in the second line; the translation brings it back into that clause.

Textual verification / uncertain: Variant reading: the Gita Press has सा देवी तां ततः प्राह; GRETIL and Freund have तां च देवीं ततः प्राह. The kept reading is construed with सा देवी as the displaced subject of यत्र आस्ते; it is grammatical but unusual in order, and the other reading is smoother. Status in corrections.json: Textual Verification Required.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • स तत्र sa tatra → सः + तत्र

    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)

    The visarga of सः is dropped before a consonant.

  • यत्रास्ते yatrāste → यत्र + आस्ते

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • शैलोद्देशेऽतिशोभने śailoddeśe'tiśobhane → शैलोद्देशे + अतिशोभने

    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)

    -e + a- → -e’- (avagraha). Inside the compound, शैल + उद्देश → शैलोद्देश by guṇa (6.1.87).

  • तां ततः प्राह tāṃ tataḥ prāha → ताम् + ततः + प्राह

    anusvāra sandhi; visarga before p · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; before the unvoiced p the visarga of ततः stays visarga.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शैलोद्देशे śailoddeśe

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    शैल + उद्देश
    Vigraha
    शैलस्य उद्देशः, तस्मिन् śailasya uddeśaḥ, tasmin
    Literally
    “in a region of the mountain”
    In the verse
    on a spot of the Himālaya

  • अतिशोभने atiśobhane

    prādi tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अति + शोभन
    Vigraha
    अतिशयेन शोभनः, तस्मिन् atiśayena śobhanaḥ, tasmin
    Literally
    “exceedingly beautiful”
    In the verse
    most lovely
Morphology & anvaya

  • प्राह prāha

    आह is the perfect of the defective root √अह् ‘say’, used with present or past sense; with प्र: प्राह ‘spoke, said’. It takes the person addressed in the accusative (ताम्).

  • गिरा girā

    Instrumental singular of the r-stem गिर् ‘voice, speech’ (nom. गीः).

  • आस्ते āste

    Present tense in a past narrative (‘where she was staying’), as often in a relative clause of place.

Anvaya · prose order

सः, यत्र अतिशोभने शैलोद्देशे सा देवी आस्ते, तत्र गत्वा, ततः तां मधुरया गिरा श्लक्ष्णं प्राह।

saḥ, yatra atiśobhane śailoddeśe sā devī āste, tatra gatvā, tataḥ tāṃ madhurayā girā ślakṣṇaṃ prāha |

Main clause सः … प्राह; an absolutive (तत्र गत्वा) with a relative clause of place (यत्र … आस्ते) whose subject सा देवी stands in the second line.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The envoy reaches the Goddess on the mountain and speaks to her softly. His gentleness belongs to the instructions he was given (5.103: bring her ‘with good will’).

Traditional interpretation

Text: GRETIL and Freund read तां च देवीं ततः प्राह ‘and then he spoke to that Devī’, which makes the second line a simple object clause. The Gita Press reading सा देवी तां ततः प्राह is kept here, with सा देवी as the subject of यत्रास्ते and तां resuming her as the person addressed (corrections.json, variants: ‘Textual Verification Required’).

Verse 5.105

Speaker
‹›

दूत उवाच॥⁠१०५⁠॥

dūta uvāca ||

दूतः · उवाच

dūtaḥ · uvāca

Word by word

  1. दूतःdūtaḥthe messenger (Sugrīva)m. nom. sg. of दूत; subject of उवाच
  2. उवाचuvācasaid√वच् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, kartari prayoga

Translation

The messenger said:

In readable EnglishThe messenger said:

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • दूत उवाच dūta uvāca → दूतः + उवाच

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -aḥ before a vowel other than a: the visarga becomes y, the y is dropped, and the two vowels stand apart (दूत | उवाच), as in मार्कण्डेय उवाच (1.1).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

No compound in this verse needs analysis.

Morphology & anvaya

Anvaya · prose order

दूतः उवाच।

dūtaḥ uvāca |

Subject and verb: a speaker label.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Sugrīva, Śumbha’s envoy, begins his formal address to the Goddess on the mountain. His speech runs to 5.114.

Verse 5.106

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

देवि दैत्येश्वरः शुम्भस्त्रैलोक्ये परमेश्वरः।दूतोऽहं प्रेषितस्तेन त्वत्सकाशमिहागतः॥⁠१०६⁠॥

devi daityeśvaraḥ śumbhastrailokye parameśvaraḥ |dūto'haṃ preṣitastena tvatsakāśamihāgataḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

देवि · दैत्येश्वरः · शुम्भः · त्रैलोक्ये · परमेश्वरःदूतः · अहम् · प्रेषितः · तेन · त्वत्सकाशम् · इह · आगतः

devi · daityeśvaraḥ · śumbhaḥ · trailokye · parameśvaraḥdūtaḥ · aham · preṣitaḥ · tena · tvatsakāśam · iha · āgataḥ

Word by word

  1. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  2. दैत्येश्वरःdaityeśvaraḥlord of the daityasm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to शुम्भः
  3. शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject (with अस्ति understood)
  4. त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (see 5.93)
  5. परमेश्वरःparameśvaraḥthe supreme lordm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate
  6. दूतःdūtaḥa messengerm. nom. sg. of दूत; predicate of अहम्
  7. अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.
  8. प्रेषितःpreṣitaḥsentpast participle (kta) of the causative of प्र + √इष्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies अहम्
  9. तेनtenaby himpronoun तद्, m. instr. sg.; agent of प्रेषितः
  10. त्वत्सकाशम्tvatsakāśaminto your presencem. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); goal of आगतः
  11. इहihahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  12. आगतःāgataḥhave comepast participle (kta) of आ + √गम्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies अहम्

Translation

O Devī, Śumbha, lord of the daityas, is the supreme lord in the three worlds. I, a messenger sent by him, have come here into your presence.

In readable English‘Goddess, Śumbha, lord of the demons, is supreme sovereign of the three worlds. I am his envoy, sent by him, and have come here before you.’

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • शुम्भस्त्रैलोक्ये śumbhastrailokye → शुम्भः + त्रैलोक्ये

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s.

  • दूतोऽहं dūto'haṃ → दूतः + अहम्

    visarga sandhi, then vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · अतो रोरप्लुतादप्लुते (6.1.113), आद्गुणः (6.1.87), एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)

    -aḥ before a becomes -o and the following a is elided (avagraha); अहम् then takes anusvāra before p (8.3.23).

  • प्रेषितस्तेन preṣitastena → प्रेषितः + तेन

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s.

  • त्वत्सकाशमिहागतः tvatsakāśamihāgataḥ → त्वत्सकाशम् + इह + आगतः

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    इह + आगतः: a + ā → ā; the m of त्वत्सकाशम् stays m before the vowel and is written joined.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • दैत्येश्वरः daityeśvaraḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    दैत्य + ईश्वर
    Vigraha
    दैत्यानाम् ईश्वरः daityānām īśvaraḥ
    Literally
    “lord of the daityas”
    In the verse
    Śumbha; a + ī → e by guṇa (6.1.87)

  • परमेश्वरः parameśvaraḥ

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    परम + ईश्वर
    Vigraha
    परमः ईश्वरः paramaḥ īśvaraḥ
    Literally
    “supreme lord”
    In the verse
    the highest ruler — Śumbha’s claim for himself

  • त्वत्सकाशम् tvatsakāśam

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (त्वद् is the compound stem of युष्मद् in the singular)

    Members
    त्वद् + सकाश
    Vigraha
    तव सकाशम् tava sakāśam
    Literally
    “your vicinity”
    In the verse
    into your presence
Morphology & anvaya

  • प्रेषितः preṣitaḥ

    Past participle of the causative प्रेषय- (प्र + √इष्): the causative -aya- is dropped before -ita. Compare प्रेषयामास (5.102).

  • आगतः āgataḥ

    Past participle of आ + √गम् with active sense for a verb of motion: ‘(I) have come’.

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, दैत्येश्वरः शुम्भः त्रैलोक्ये परमेश्वरः (अस्ति)। तेन प्रेषितः दूतः अहं त्वत्सकाशम् इह आगतः (अस्मि)।

devi, daityeśvaraḥ śumbhaḥ trailokye parameśvaraḥ (asti) | tena preṣitaḥ dūtaḥ ahaṃ tvatsakāśam iha āgataḥ (asmi) |

Two nominal sentences with the copula understood: Śumbha’s status, then the messenger’s own (अहम् with दूतः, प्रेषितः, आगतः).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Sugrīva opens with protocol: he names his master’s title and his own office. Śumbha is called परमेश्वरः, ‘supreme lord’, in the three worlds.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: the title is ironic in context. The gods’ hymn has just praised the Goddess as the power within all beings, and the one the envoy addresses as ‘Devī’ is the sovereign he does not recognise.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) names the Goddess परमेश्वरी (name 396), the feminine of the title Śumbha claims here. The comparison is ours; the Devī Māhātmya itself makes the irony through its story.

Verse 5.107

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

अव्याहताज्ञः सर्वासु यः सदा देवयोनिषु।निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः स यदाह शृणुष्व तत्॥⁠१०७⁠॥

avyāhatājñaḥ sarvāsu yaḥ sadā devayoniṣu |nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ sa yadāha śṛṇuṣva tat ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

अव्याहताज्ञः · सर्वासु · यः · सदा · देवयोनिषुनिर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः · सः · यत् · आह · शृणुष्व · तत्

avyāhatājñaḥ · sarvāsu · yaḥ · sadā · devayoniṣunirjitākhiladaityāriḥ · saḥ · yat · āha · śṛṇuṣva · tat

Word by word

  1. अव्याहताज्ञःavyāhatājñaḥwhose command is unobstructedm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of यः
  2. सर्वासुsarvāsuamong allpronominal adjective सर्व, f. loc. pl.; qualifies देवयोनिषु
  3. यःyaḥwhorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; refers to Śumbha; its correlative is सः
  4. सदाsadāalwaysindeclinable (adverb of time)
  5. देवयोनिषुdevayoniṣuamong the divine orders of beingsf. loc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); locative of the sphere
  6. निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिःnirjitākhiladaityāriḥwho has conquered all the enemies of the Daityas (the gods)m. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); second predicate of the same subject
  7. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; correlative of यः; subject of आह
  8. यत्yatwhatrelative pronoun यद्, n. acc. sg.; object of आह
  9. आहāhasays√ब्रू (adādi 2U ‘to speak’), the special form आह (laṭ, present, with perfect-type endings), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  10. शृणुष्वśṛṇuṣvahear, listen to√श्रु (svādi 5 ‘to hear’, stem शृणु-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada (an epic use; classical Sanskrit has the parasmaipada शृणु)
  11. तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. acc. sg.; correlative of यत्; object of शृणुष्व

Translation

He who always (has) unobstructed command among all the divine orders, (and) who has conquered all the enemies of the Daityas — hear that which he says.

In readable EnglishAmong all the orders of heavenly beings his command is never resisted, and he has vanquished every foe of the Daityas. Hear what he says.

The relative clause is turned into two plain statements.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ → अ + वि + आहत + आज्ञः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ and savarṇa-dīrgha) · इको यणचि (6.1.77); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    i before ā becomes y (वि + आहत → व्याहत); a + ā → ā (व्याहत + आज्ञा → व्याहताज्ञ).

  • निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ → निर्जित + अखिल + दैत्य + अरिः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    Twice a + a → ā: निर्जिताखिल, दैत्यारि.

  • स यदाह sa yadāha → सः + यत् + आह

    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः); consonant sandhi (voicing) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    The visarga of सः is dropped before the consonant y; the final t of यत् becomes d before the vowel of आह.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ

    bahuvrīhi (its first member अव्याहत is a nañ tatpuruṣa: न व्याहता)

    Members
    अव्याहत + आज्ञा
    Vigraha
    अव्याहता आज्ञा यस्य सः avyāhatā ājñā yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “one whose command is not struck down”
    In the verse
    a ruler whose orders no one resists

  • देवयोनिषु devayoniṣu

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    देव + योनि
    Vigraha
    देवानां योनयः, तासु devānāṃ yonayaḥ, tāsu
    Literally
    “among the kinds (births) of the gods”
    In the verse
    among all the orders of superhuman beings
    Alternative
    देवयोनि is often a bahuvrīhi, देवः योनिः येषां ते ‘beings of divine origin’ (masculine). Here the feminine सर्वासु shows that योनि ‘kind, birth’ (f.) is the head, so the tatpuruṣa fits.

  • निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ

    bahuvrīhi (inside it दैत्यारि is a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, दैत्यानाम् अरिः)

    Members
    निर्जित + अखिल + दैत्यारि
    Vigraha
    निर्जिताः अखिलाः दैत्यारयः येन सः nirjitāḥ akhilāḥ daityārayaḥ yena saḥ
    Literally
    “one by whom all the Daitya-enemies have been conquered”
    In the verse
    the conqueror of all the gods
    Alternative
    दैत्यारि ‘enemy of the Daityas’ can name Viṣṇu alone; with अखिल ‘all’ the whole body of gods is meant.
Morphology & anvaya

  • आह āha

    √ब्रू has in the present the substitute आह with perfect-type endings (आह, आहतुः, आहुः); Pāṇini allows it optionally beside ब्रवीति. The sense is present: ‘(he) says’.

  • शृणुष्व śṛṇuṣva

    Imperative ātmanepada of √श्रु. The root is parasmaipada in Pāṇini; the ātmanepada imperative is common in the epics and Purāṇas, with no difference of sense.

  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ

    The feminine आज्ञा is shortened to आज्ञ as the last member of a bahuvrīhi that qualifies a masculine: ‘he whose command …’.

Anvaya · prose order

यः सदा सर्वासु देवयोनिषु अव्याहताज्ञः, निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः सः (शुम्भः) यत् आह, तत् शृणुष्व।

yaḥ sadā sarvāsu devayoniṣu avyāhatājñaḥ, nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ saḥ (śumbhaḥ) yat āha, tat śṛṇuṣva |

A relative clause (यः … अव्याहताज्ञः) and a second epithet describe the subject सः; inside the main clause a second relative pair (यत् … तत्) makes the object of the imperative शृणुष्व.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The messenger Sugrīva, sent by Śumbha (5.102–104), finishes introducing his master (5.106) before delivering the message. The two titles summarise the opening of the chapter: the brothers have taken the gods’ offices and driven them out (5.2–5).

Traditional interpretation

देवयोनि is the usual name for the superhuman orders below the gods proper; the lexicon Amarakośa lists ten (vidyādharas, apsarases, yakṣas, rakṣases, gandharvas, kinnaras, piśācas, guhyakas, siddhas and bhūtas). The claim is that every such order obeys Śumbha.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: the message is preceded by credentials. Power announces itself before it asks, so that the asking sounds like a favour.

Verse 5.108

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

मम त्रैलोक्यमखिलं मम देवा वशानुगाः।यज्ञभागानहं सर्वानुपाश्नामि पृथक् पृथक्॥⁠१०८⁠॥

mama trailokyamakhilaṃ mama devā vaśānugāḥ |yajñabhāgānahaṃ sarvānupāśnāmi pṛthak pṛthak ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

मम · त्रैलोक्यम् · अखिलम् · मम · देवाः · वशानुगाःयज्ञभागान् · अहम् · सर्वान् · उपाश्नामि · पृथक् · पृथक्

mama · trailokyam · akhilam · mama · devāḥ · vaśānugāḥyajñabhāgān · aham · sarvān · upāśnāmi · pṛthak · pṛthak

Word by word

  1. ममmamamine, of mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; predicative possessive (‘is mine’)
  2. त्रैलोक्यम्trailokyamthe three worldsn. nom. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (taddhita from त्रिलोकी); subject (verb ‘is’ understood)
  3. अखिलम्akhilamentire, wholeadjective अखिल, n. nom. sg.; qualifies त्रैलोक्यम्
  4. ममmamamy, to mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; depends on वशानुगाः
  5. देवाःdevāḥthe godsm. nom. pl. of देव; subject
  6. वशानुगाःvaśānugāḥobedient, following (my) willm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of देवाः
  7. यज्ञभागान्yajñabhāgānthe shares of the sacrificem. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of उपाश्नामि
  8. अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
  9. सर्वान्sarvānallpronominal adjective सर्व, m. acc. pl.; qualifies यज्ञभागान्
  10. उपाश्नामिupāśnāmiI enjoy, I consumeउप + √अश् (kryādi 9P ‘to eat’, stem अश्ना-), laṭ (present), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  11. पृथक्pṛthakseparatelyindeclinable (adverb)
  12. पृथक्pṛthak(each) separatelyindeclinable; repeated (āmreḍita) to express distribution: ‘each one apart’

Translation

Mine is the entire triple world; mine are the gods, following my will. All the shares of the sacrifice I enjoy, each separately.

In readable English“The three worlds are mine entire, and the gods obey my will. Every share of the sacrifice, one by one, is mine to enjoy.

The opening quotation mark shows that the message is quoted in Śumbha’s own words.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • त्रैलोक्यमखिलं मम trailokyamakhilaṃ mama → त्रैलोक्यम् + अखिलम् + मम

    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m stays m before the vowel of अखिलम् and is simply written with it; before the consonant of मम it becomes anusvāra.

  • देवा वशानुगाः devā vaśānugāḥ → देवाः + वशानुगाः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -āḥ before a voiced sound: the visarga becomes y and the y is dropped, leaving -ā.

  • यज्ञभागानहं yajñabhāgānahaṃ → यज्ञभागान् + अहम्

    no change (final n before a vowel) · ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) does not apply

    The n is not doubled, because the vowel before it (ā) is long; it is simply joined to अहम्. The same in सर्वानुपाश्नामि.

  • वशानुगाः vaśānugāḥ → वश + अनुगाः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • वशानुगाः vaśānugāḥ

    upapada tatpuruṣa (अनुग = अनु + √गम् + ḍa)

    Members
    वश + अनुग
    Vigraha
    वशम् अनुगच्छन्ति इति वशानुगाः vaśam anugacchanti iti vaśānugāḥ
    Literally
    “those who follow the will”
    In the verse
    subject to my command

  • यज्ञभागान् yajñabhāgān

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    यज्ञ + भाग
    Vigraha
    यज्ञस्य भागाः, तान् yajñasya bhāgāḥ, tān
    Literally
    “the portions of the sacrifice”
    In the verse
    the offerings due to each god at the rite

  • त्रैलोक्यम् trailokyam

    samāhāra dvigu (त्रिलोकी), made into a noun with the taddhita ṣyañ that adds no new sense

    Members
    त्रि + लोक (+ taddhita ṣyañ)
    Vigraha
    त्रयाणां लोकानां समाहारः त्रिलोकी, सा एव त्रैलोक्यम् trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ samāhāraḥ trilokī, sā eva trailokyam
    Literally
    “the group of three worlds”
    In the verse
    the whole universe: earth, the middle region and heaven
Morphology & anvaya

  • उपाश्नामि upāśnāmi

    √अश् ‘to eat’ belongs to the ninth class: अश् + ना → अश्नामि. With उप the prefix and the root vowel merge (उप + अश्नामि → उपाश्नामि). The verb of eating is chosen for the sacrificial shares, which the gods ‘eat’.

  • पृथक् pṛthak

    The repeated adverb (पृथक् पृथक्) expresses distribution, ‘one by one, each share apart’ — every god’s portion now goes to Śumbha.

Anvaya · prose order

अखिलं त्रैलोक्यं मम (अस्ति); देवाः मम वशानुगाः (सन्ति); अहं सर्वान् यज्ञभागान् पृथक् पृथक् उपाश्नामि।

akhilaṃ trailokyaṃ mama (asti); devāḥ mama vaśānugāḥ (santi); ahaṃ sarvān yajñabhāgān pṛthak pṛthak upāśnāmi |

Three clauses, the first two nominal (the verb ‘is / are’ understood), the third with the finite verb उपाश्नामि. From here to 5.114 the messenger speaks in Śumbha’s first person.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The message proper begins, in Śumbha’s voice. Its claims repeat the narrator’s account at the head of the chapter: the three worlds and the sacrificial shares were taken from Indra (5.2), and the gods were driven out (5.4–5).

Traditional interpretation

The sacrificial share (यज्ञभाग) is the gods’ sustenance and the sign of their office. In the Purāṇic view, a demon who eats the shares has overturned the order of the world, not merely won a war; the Devī’s own message in 8.26 demands that the gods again be ‘eaters of oblations’ (हविर्भुजः), and 12.34 records that they enjoyed their shares once more.

Verse 5.109

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

त्रैलोक्ये वररत्नानि मम वश्यान्यशेषतः।तथैव गजरत्नं च हृत्वा देवेन्द्रवाहनम्॥⁠१०९⁠॥

trailokye vararatnāni mama vaśyānyaśeṣataḥ |tathaiva gajaratnaṃ ca hṛtvā devendravāhanam ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

त्रैलोक्ये · वररत्नानि · मम · वश्यानि · अशेषतःतथा · एव · गजरत्नम् · च · हृत्वा · देवेन्द्रवाहनम्

trailokye · vararatnāni · mama · vaśyāni · aśeṣataḥtathā · eva · gajaratnam · ca · hṛtvā · devendravāhanam

Word by word

  1. त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य
  2. वररत्नानिvararatnānithe choicest jewelsn. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject
  3. ममmamato mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with वश्यानि ‘subject to me’
  4. वश्यानिvaśyānisubject, under controladjective वश्य (वश + taddhita yat), n. nom. pl.; predicate of वररत्नानि
  5. अशेषतःaśeṣataḥwithout exception, entirelyindeclinable (अशेष + tasil, adverb)
  6. तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  7. एवevajust, indeedindeclinable (emphatic particle); तथा एव ‘in just the same way’
  8. गजरत्नम्gajaratnamthe jewel of elephantsn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of हृत्वा
  9. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  10. हृत्वाhṛtvāhaving seizedabsolutive (ktvā) of √हृ (bhvādi 1U ‘to take, carry off’)
  11. देवेन्द्रवाहनम्devendravāhanamthe mount of the lord of the gods (Indra)n. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to गजरत्नम्

Translation

In the three worlds the choice jewels are subject to me without exception; and likewise the jewel of elephants, the mount of the lord of the gods, having seized (it) —

In readable EnglishEvery treasure of worth in the three worlds belongs to me, without exception — so too the king of elephants, Indra’s own mount, which I have taken.

The absolutive हृत्वा is rendered as a relative clause ‘which I have taken’, completing the loose syntax.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • वश्यान्यशेषतः vaśyānyaśeṣataḥ → वश्यानि + अशेषतः

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    Final i before a different vowel becomes y.

  • तथैव tathaiva → तथा + एव

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    ā + e → ai.

  • देवेन्द्रवाहनम् devendravāhanam → देव + इन्द्र + वाहनम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + i → e.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • वररत्नानि vararatnāni

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    वर + रत्न
    Vigraha
    वराणि च तानि रत्नानि च varāṇi ca tāni ratnāni ca
    Literally
    “excellent jewels”
    In the verse
    the finest treasures of every kind
    Alternative
    Also read as a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, रत्नानां वराणि ‘the best of jewels’; the sense is the same.

  • गजरत्नम् gajaratnam

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (of selection: ‘the jewel among elephants’)

    Members
    गज + रत्न
    Vigraha
    गजानां रत्नम् gajānāṃ ratnam
    Literally
    “the jewel of elephants”
    In the verse
    the finest of elephants, Airāvata (named in 5.94)

  • देवेन्द्रवाहनम् devendravāhanam

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, whose first member is also a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    देवेन्द्र + वाहन
    Vigraha
    देवेन्द्रस्य वाहनम् (देवेन्द्रः = देवानाम् इन्द्रः) devendrasya vāhanam (devendraḥ = devānām indraḥ)
    Literally
    “the vehicle of the chief of the gods”
    In the verse
    Indra’s mount
Morphology & anvaya

  • हृत्वा hṛtvā

    Absolutive of √हृ (no prefix, so -त्वा, not -य). It has no finite verb of its own in the verse: the sense ‘(is mine), having been seized by me’ is carried over from मम वश्यानि. A loose construction typical of reported speech.

  • वश्यानि vaśyāni

    वश ‘control’ + the taddhita -य: ‘what is in (one’s) control’. With the genitive मम: ‘subject to me’.

Anvaya · prose order

त्रैलोक्ये (यानि) वररत्नानि (तानि) अशेषतः मम वश्यानि (सन्ति); तथा एव देवेन्द्रवाहनं गजरत्नं च (मया) हृत्वा (मम वश्यम्)।

trailokye (yāni) vararatnāni (tāni) aśeṣataḥ mama vaśyāni (santi); tathā eva devendravāhanaṃ gajaratnaṃ ca (mayā) hṛtvā (mama vaśyam) |

The first line is a nominal sentence (verb understood). The second hangs on the absolutive हृत्वा and borrows its predicate from the first: the elephant too is ‘subject to me’.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Śumbha’s message repeats what Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa told him (5.93–94): the jewels of the three worlds are in his palace, and Indra’s elephant Airāvata among them. The messenger turns their report into a boast.

Traditional interpretation

रत्न ‘jewel’ in this passage means the best of any kind — elephant, horse, tree, woman — not only gems. The list prepares the inference of 5.112: if all jewels are his, the ‘jewel of women’ must be his too.

Verse 5.110

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतमश्वरत्नं ममामरैः।उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञं तत्प्रणिपत्य समर्पितम्॥⁠११०⁠॥

kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamaśvaratnaṃ mamāmaraiḥ |uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñaṃ tatpraṇipatya samarpitam ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् · अश्वरत्नम् · मम · अमरैःउच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् · तत् · प्रणिपत्य · समर्पितम्

kṣīrodamathanodbhūtam · aśvaratnam · mama · amaraiḥuccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam · tat · praṇipatya · samarpitam

Word by word

  1. क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम्kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamthat arose from the churning of the Milk Oceann. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अश्वरत्नम्
  2. अश्वरत्नम्aśvaratnamthe jewel of horsesn. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of the passive समर्पितम्
  3. ममmamato mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg., used for the dative (recipient) with समर्पितम्
  4. अमरैःamaraiḥby the immortals (the gods)m. instr. pl. of अमर; agent of समर्पितम्
  5. उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम्uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñamnamed Uccaiḥśravasn. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अश्वरत्नम्
  6. तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies अश्वरत्नम्
  7. प्रणिपत्यpraṇipatyahaving bowed downabsolutive (lyap) of प्र + नि + √पत् (bhvādi 1P ‘to fall’); its subject is the agent अमरैः
  8. समर्पितम्samarpitamwas handed over, was surrenderedpast passive participle (kta) of सम् + √ऋ, causative stem अर्पय-, n. nom. sg.; predicate

Translation

That jewel of horses called Uccaiḥśravas, which arose from the churning of the Milk Ocean, was handed over to me by the immortals, having bowed down.

In readable EnglishUccaiḥśravas, the finest of horses, born from the churning of the Ocean of Milk, the gods themselves surrendered to me with bowed heads.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतमश्वरत्नं kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamaśvaratnaṃ → क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् + अश्वरत्नम्

    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m is joined to the vowel of अश्वरत्नम्; the m of अश्वरत्नम् becomes anusvāra before मम.

  • ममामरैः mamāmaraiḥ → मम + अमरैः

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूत kṣīrodamathanodbhūta → क्षीरोद + मथन + उद्भूत

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + u → o (मथनोद्भूत).

  • तत्प्रणिपत्य tatpraṇipatya → तत् + प्रणिपत्य

    no change · खरि च (8.4.55)

    Final t stays unvoiced before the unvoiced p; the words are only written together.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् kṣīrodamathanodbhūtam

    pañcamī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (क्षीरोदमथन)

    Members
    क्षीरोदमथन + उद्भूत
    Vigraha
    क्षीरोदस्य मथनम् क्षीरोदमथनम्; क्षीरोदमथनात् उद्भूतम् kṣīrodasya mathanam kṣīrodamathanam; kṣīrodamathanāt udbhūtam
    Literally
    “arisen from the churning of the milk-water (ocean)”
    In the verse
    one of the treasures that came up when gods and asuras churned the ocean
    Alternative
    क्षीरोद itself is a bahuvrīhi, क्षीरम् उदकं यस्य सः ‘that whose water is milk’, with उदक shortened to उद.

  • अश्वरत्नम् aśvaratnam

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (of selection)

    Members
    अश्व + रत्न
    Vigraha
    अश्वानां रत्नम् aśvānāṃ ratnam
    Literally
    “the jewel of horses”
    In the verse
    the finest of horses

  • उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    उच्चैःश्रवस + संज्ञा
    Vigraha
    उच्चैःश्रवसः संज्ञा यस्य तत् uccaiḥśravasaḥ saṃjñā yasya tat
    Literally
    “having the name Uccaiḥśravas”
    In the verse
    the horse called Uccaiḥśravas
    Alternative
    The name उच्चैःश्रवस् is itself a bahuvrīhi, उच्चैः श्रवसी यस्य सः ‘whose ears are high’ (or ‘whose neigh is loud’).
Morphology & anvaya

  • समर्पितम् samarpitam

    Past passive participle of the causative of सम् + √ऋ (‘cause to go to’ = ‘hand over’): सम्-अर्पित. With the instrumental अमरैः it is a passive sentence: ‘(it) was surrendered by the gods’.

  • प्रणिपत्य praṇipatya

    Absolutive with prefixes, so -य (lyap). In a passive sentence its subject is the agent in the instrumental: the gods bowed, then handed over the horse.

  • उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam

    The horse’s name is the s-stem उच्चैःश्रवस्; in this compound it appears as उच्चैःश्रवस-, as if an a-stem, a by-form found in epic and Purāṇic usage. The visarga of उच्चैः is kept inside the compound, as printed.

Anvaya · prose order

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञं तत् अश्वरत्नम् अमरैः प्रणिपत्य मम समर्पितम्।

kṣīrodamathanodbhūtam uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñaṃ tat aśvaratnam amaraiḥ praṇipatya mama samarpitam |

A passive sentence: subject अश्वरत्नम् with three qualifiers, agent अमरैः, recipient मम, predicate समर्पितम्; the absolutive प्रणिपत्य belongs to the agent.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The second of Indra’s great treasures: the horse Uccaiḥśravas, already listed by Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa (5.94). Śumbha adds a detail they did not give — the gods bowed as they handed it over.

Traditional interpretation

Uccaiḥśravas is one of the treasures that rose from the ocean when the gods and asuras churned it, and belongs to Indra in the Purāṇic accounts. A treasure won by the gods from that churning is now given up to an asura: the boast measures how complete his victory is.

Verse 5.111

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

यानि चान्यानि देवेषु गन्धर्वेषूरगेषु च।रत्नभूतानि भूतानि तानि मय्येव शोभने॥⁠१११⁠॥

yāni cānyāni deveṣu gandharveṣūrageṣu ca |ratnabhūtāni bhūtāni tāni mayyeva śobhane ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यानि · च · अन्यानि · देवेषु · गन्धर्वेषु · उरगेषु · चरत्नभूतानि · भूतानि · तानि · मयि · एव · शोभने

yāni · ca · anyāni · deveṣu · gandharveṣu · urageṣu · caratnabhūtāni · bhūtāni · tāni · mayi · eva · śobhane

Word by word

  1. यानिyāniwhich, whateverrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. pl.; qualifies भूतानि; correlative तानि
  2. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); joins this verse to the list before it
  3. अन्यानिanyāniotherpronominal adjective अन्य, n. nom. pl.; qualifies भूतानि
  4. देवेषुdeveṣuamong the godsm. loc. pl. of देव
  5. गन्धर्वेषुgandharveṣuamong the Gandharvasm. loc. pl. of गन्धर्व
  6. उरगेषुurageṣuamong the serpents (Nāgas)m. loc. pl. of उरग (‘going on the breast’: उरस् + √गम् + ḍa)
  7. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); closes the list
  8. रत्नभूतानिratnabhūtānithat are jewels, of jewel rankn. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies भूतानि
  9. भूतानिbhūtānithings, beingsn. nom. pl. of भूत (noun: ‘what has come to be’); subject of the relative clause
  10. तानिtānithey, thosepronoun तद्, n. nom. pl.; correlative of यानि; subject of the main clause
  11. मयिmayiin me, with mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, loc. sg.
  12. एवevaalone, onlyindeclinable (restrictive particle)
  13. शोभनेśobhaneO beautiful onef. voc. sg. of शोभना (√शुभ् + lyuṭ, ‘shining, lovely’); addresses the Devī

Translation

And whatever other things that are jewels (there are) among the gods, the Gandharvas and the serpents — they (are) in me alone, O beautiful one.

In readable EnglishAnd whatever else ranks as a jewel among gods, Gandharvas and serpents — all of it, lovely one, rests with me alone.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • चान्यानि cānyāni → च + अन्यानि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • गन्धर्वेषूरगेषु gandharveṣūrageṣu → गन्धर्वेषु + उरगेषु

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    u + u → ū.

  • मय्येव mayyeva → मयि + एव

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    The final i of मयि becomes y before e, so the y of the stem and the new y stand together (मय्येव).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • रत्नभूतानि ratnabhūtāni

    karmadhāraya (भूत ‘being, become’ as final member)

    Members
    रत्न + भूत
    Vigraha
    रत्नानि च तानि भूतानि च ratnāni ca tāni bhūtāni ca
    Literally
    “that have become jewels, that are jewels”
    In the verse
    treasures of the first rank among their kind
Morphology & anvaya

  • भूतानि bhūtāni

    The same word stands twice in a row with two functions: in रत्नभूतानि ‘being jewels’ it is the participle of √भू; alone, भूतानि is the noun ‘things, beings’. The play (रत्नभूतानि भूतानि) is deliberate: ‘things that are jewels’.

  • शोभने śobhane

    Vocative of the feminine शोभना: the -ā stem has -e in the vocative singular. The flattering address prepares the proposal of 5.112.

Anvaya · prose order

शोभने, देवेषु गन्धर्वेषु उरगेषु च यानि च अन्यानि रत्नभूतानि भूतानि (सन्ति), तानि मयि एव (सन्ति)।

śobhane, deveṣu gandharveṣu urageṣu ca yāni ca anyāni ratnabhūtāni bhūtāni (santi), tāni mayi eva (santi) |

A relative clause (यानि … भूतानि) names the subject; the main clause तानि मयि एव is nominal, with ‘are’ understood.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The list of treasures closes with a general claim: every ‘jewel’ of every race is now in Śumbha’s possession. The vocative शोभने turns the boast towards the Devī.

Traditional interpretation

The Devī is addressed first as ‘beautiful’; in the next verse she is placed among the jewels. The argument is the one Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa made (5.100): all jewels are his, so why not this one?

Verse 5.112

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

स्त्रीरत्नभूतां त्वां देवि लोके मन्यामहे वयम्।सा त्वमस्मानुपागच्छ यतो रत्नभुजो वयम्॥⁠११२⁠॥

strīratnabhūtāṃ tvāṃ devi loke manyāmahe vayam |sā tvamasmānupāgaccha yato ratnabhujo vayam ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् · त्वाम् · देवि · लोके · मन्यामहे · वयम्सा · त्वम् · अस्मान् · उपागच्छ · यतः · रत्नभुजः · वयम्

strīratnabhūtām · tvām · devi · loke · manyāmahe · vayamsā · tvam · asmān · upāgaccha · yataḥ · ratnabhujaḥ · vayam

Word by word

  1. स्त्रीरत्नभूताम्strīratnabhūtāmbeing the jewel among womenf. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with मन्यामहे
  2. त्वाम्tvāmyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, acc. sg.; object of मन्यामहे
  3. देविdeviO Devī, O ladyf. voc. sg. of देवी
  4. लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
  5. मन्यामहेmanyāmahewe consider, we regard√मन् (divādi 4Ā ‘to think’), laṭ (present), uttama-puruṣa bahuvacana, ātmanepada
  6. वयम्vayamwefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. pl.; subject (the royal plural)
  7. साsāsuch (as you are), that onepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; resumes the description: ‘you, being such’
  8. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of उपागच्छ
  9. अस्मान्asmānto usfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. pl.; object of उपागच्छ (goal of motion)
  10. उपागच्छupāgacchacome to, approachउप + आ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  11. यतःyataḥsince, becauseindeclinable (यद् + tasil; causal conjunction)
  12. रत्नभुजःratnabhujaḥenjoyers of jewelsm. nom. pl. of रत्नभुज्; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of वयम्
  13. वयम्vayamwefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. pl.; subject

Translation

We regard you, O Devī, as being the jewel among women in the world. Such as you are, come to us, since we are the enjoyers of jewels.

In readable EnglishWe hold you, O Devī, to be the jewel among the women of the world. Come to us, then, for jewels are ours to enjoy.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • त्वमस्मानुपागच्छ tvamasmānupāgaccha → त्वम् + अस्मान् + उपागच्छ

    no change before vowels · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) and ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) do not apply

    Final m and final n (after the long ā of अस्मान्) are simply joined to the following vowel.

  • उपागच्छ upāgaccha → उप + आ + गच्छ

    vowel sandhi between prefixes (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • यतो रत्नभुजो वयम् yato ratnabhujo vayam → यतः + रत्नभुजः + वयम्

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before a voiced consonant (r, v) becomes -o, twice.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् strīratnabhūtām

    karmadhāraya with भूत ‘being’, containing a saptamī tatpuruṣa (स्त्रीरत्न, of selection)

    Members
    स्त्रीरत्न + भूत
    Vigraha
    स्त्रीषु रत्नम् स्त्रीरत्नम्; स्त्रीरत्नं भूता, ताम् strīṣu ratnam strīratnam; strīratnaṃ bhūtā, tām
    Literally
    “who has become the jewel among women”
    In the verse
    the finest of all women
    Alternative
    स्त्रीरत्न may also be read as a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, स्त्रीणां रत्नम्; the sense is the same.

  • रत्नभुजः ratnabhujaḥ

    upapada tatpuruṣa (√भुज् + kvip)

    Members
    रत्न + भुज्
    Vigraha
    रत्नानि भुञ्जते इति रत्नभुजः ratnāni bhuñjate iti ratnabhujaḥ
    Literally
    “jewel-enjoyers”
    In the verse
    those to whom every treasure belongs by right
Morphology & anvaya

  • मन्यामहे manyāmahe

    Present ātmanepada of √मन्, fourth class (मन्य-): ‘we think, regard’. With two accusatives: त्वाम् (object) and स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् (what she is regarded as).

  • रत्नभुजः ratnabhujaḥ

    Nominative plural of the root-noun रत्नभुज् (j-final): the bare root with zero affix (kvip) as the final member of an upapada compound, like ‘-bhuj’ in भूभुज् ‘enjoyer of the earth, king’.

  • सा sā

    The pronoun सा resumes the predicate of the first line: ‘you, being such (a jewel)’. सा त्वम् is a common Purāṇic opening for a request.

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, वयं लोके त्वां स्त्रीरत्नभूतां मन्यामहे। यतः वयं रत्नभुजः, (अतः) सा त्वम् अस्मान् उपागच्छ।

devi, vayaṃ loke tvāṃ strīratnabhūtāṃ manyāmahe | yataḥ vayaṃ ratnabhujaḥ, (ataḥ) sā tvam asmān upāgaccha |

The first sentence has a double accusative (त्वाम् … स्त्रीरत्नभूताम्). The second is an imperative with a causal clause (यतः …) giving the reason.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The argument of the message is now explicit: every jewel belongs to Śumbha, the Devī is the jewel among women, therefore she must come to him. Śumbha speaks of himself in the plural (वयम्), as a king.

Traditional interpretation

The word रत्न has carried the whole speech from 5.109. The tradition reads the irony of the scene: the one he names a ‘jewel’ to be owned is the power by whom, in the narrator’s words of 5.116, the world itself is upheld.

Verse 5.113

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

मां वा ममानुजं वापि निशुम्भमुरुविक्रमम्।भज त्वं चञ्चलापाङ्गि रत्नभूतासि वै यतः॥⁠११३⁠॥

māṃ vā mamānujaṃ vāpi niśumbhamuruvikramam |bhaja tvaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi ratnabhūtāsi vai yataḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

माम् · वा · मम · अनुजम् · वा · अपि · निशुम्भम् · उरुविक्रमम्भज · त्वम् · चञ्चलापाङ्गि · रत्नभूता · असि · वै · यतः

mām · vā · mama · anujam · vā · api · niśumbham · uruvikramambhaja · tvam · cañcalāpāṅgi · ratnabhūtā · asi · vai · yataḥ

Word by word

  1. माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of भज
  2. वाvāeither, orindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
  3. ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with अनुजम्
  4. अनुजम्anujamyounger brotherm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of भज
  5. वाvāorindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
  6. अपिapialso, evenindeclinable (particle); वा अपि ‘or else’
  7. निशुम्भम्niśumbhamNiśumbham. acc. sg.; proper name; in apposition to अनुजम्
  8. उरुविक्रमम्uruvikramamof wide-ranging valourm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies निशुम्भम्
  9. भजbhajachoose, take (as husband); lit. ‘resort to, share in’√भज् (bhvādi 1U ‘to share, resort to’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  10. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
  11. चञ्चलापाङ्गिcañcalāpāṅgiO you of the darting glancesf. voc. sg. of चञ्चलापाङ्गी; compound (see Samāsa)
  12. रत्नभूताratnabhūtābeing a jewelf. nom. sg.; compound (रत्न + भूत, as in 5.111); predicate
  13. असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi 2P ‘to be’), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  14. वैvaiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  15. यतःyataḥsince, becauseindeclinable (causal conjunction)

Translation

Choose me, or else my younger brother Niśumbha of wide valour, O you of darting glances — since you are indeed a jewel.

In readable EnglishTake me, or my younger brother Niśumbha of mighty valour, O lady of the darting glance — for a jewel is what you are.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • ममानुजं mamānujaṃ → मम + अनुजम्

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā; the final m becomes anusvāra before वा (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

  • वापि vāpi → वा + अपि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā; likewise रत्नभूतासि (रत्नभूता + असि).

  • निशुम्भमुरुविक्रमम् niśumbhamuruvikramam → निशुम्भम् + उरुविक्रमम्

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    The m is written together with the following vowel.

  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi → चञ्चल + अपाङ्गि

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अनुजम् anujam

    upapada tatpuruṣa (अनु + √जन् + ḍa)

    Members
    अनु + ज
    Vigraha
    अनु (पश्चात्) जातः, तम् anu (paścāt) jātaḥ, tam
    Literally
    “born after”
    In the verse
    younger brother

  • उरुविक्रमम् uruvikramam

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    उरु + विक्रम
    Vigraha
    उरुः विक्रमः यस्य सः, तम् uruḥ vikramaḥ yasya saḥ, tam
    Literally
    “whose valour is wide”
    In the verse
    of great prowess

  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi

    bahuvrīhi (feminine in -ī)

    Members
    चञ्चल + अपाङ्ग
    Vigraha
    चञ्चलौ अपाङ्गौ यस्याः सा, तत्सम्बुद्धौ cañcalau apāṅgau yasyāḥ sā, tatsambuddhau
    Literally
    “she whose outer eye-corners are restless”
    In the verse
    a woman with lively, darting glances — a conventional epithet of beauty
Morphology & anvaya

  • भज bhaja

    Imperative of √भज्. Its basic sense is ‘to share, resort to’; with a man as object, of a woman, it means to choose him, give oneself to him in marriage.

  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi

    A bahuvrīhi ending in a body-part (अङ्ग) forms its feminine with -ī: चञ्चलापाङ्गी; the vocative shortens the ī: चञ्चलापाङ्गि.

Anvaya · prose order

चञ्चलापाङ्गि, यतः त्वं वै रत्नभूता असि, (अतः) मां वा मम अनुजम् उरुविक्रमं निशुम्भं वा अपि भज।

cañcalāpāṅgi, yataḥ tvaṃ vai ratnabhūtā asi, (ataḥ) māṃ vā mama anujam uruvikramaṃ niśumbhaṃ vā api bhaja |

An imperative (भज) with two alternative objects joined by वा … वा अपि, and a causal clause (यतः …) at the end.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The proposal itself: the Devī may choose either brother. The reason is the same as in 5.112 — she is a jewel, and jewels go to the brothers.

Traditional interpretation

The offer of a choice between two husbands prepares the Devī’s answer: she will lay down one condition for either of them (5.120–121). The narrative leaves both brothers in the frame; both will meet her, but only in battle (chapters 9–10).

Verse 5.114

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

परमैश्वर्यमतुलं प्राप्स्यसे मत्परिग्रहात्।एतद् बुद्ध्या समालोच्य मत्परिग्रहतां व्रज॥⁠११४⁠॥

paramaiśvaryamatulaṃ prāpsyase matparigrahāt |etad buddhyā samālocya matparigrahatāṃ vraja ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

परमैश्वर्यम् · अतुलम् · प्राप्स्यसे · मत्परिग्रहात्एतत् · बुद्ध्या · समालोच्य · मत्परिग्रहताम् · व्रज

paramaiśvaryam · atulam · prāpsyase · matparigrahātetat · buddhyā · samālocya · matparigrahatām · vraja

Word by word

  1. परमैश्वर्यम्paramaiśvaryamsupreme sovereignty, highest wealth and powern. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of प्राप्स्यसे
  2. अतुलम्atulammatchlessn. acc. sg. of अतुल; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies परमैश्वर्यम्
  3. प्राप्स्यसेprāpsyaseyou will obtainप्र + √आप् (svādi 5P ‘to reach, obtain’), lṛṭ (simple future), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada (classical प्राप्स्यसि)
  4. मत्परिग्रहात्matparigrahātfrom being taken by me (as wife)m. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of cause
  5. एतत्etatthispronoun एतद्, n. acc. sg.; object of समालोच्य
  6. बुद्ध्याbuddhyāwith (your) understandingf. instr. sg. of बुद्धि (i-stem)
  7. समालोच्यsamālocyahaving considered wellabsolutive (lyap) of सम् + आ + √लोच् (curādi 10 ‘to see, consider’)
  8. मत्परिग्रहताम्matparigrahatāmthe state of being my consortf. acc. sg. of मत्परिग्रहता (compound + abstract suffix tal); goal of व्रज
  9. व्रजvrajago to, enter (that state)√व्रज् (bhvādi 1P ‘to go’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

You will obtain supreme, matchless sovereignty from being taken by me. Having considered this well with your understanding, go to the state of being my consort.

In readable EnglishAs my wife you will gain a sovereignty beyond compare. Weigh this well in your mind, and become my consort.”

The closing quotation mark ends the message quoted in Śumbha’s words (from 5.108).

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • परमैश्वर्यमतुलं paramaiśvaryamatulaṃ → परमैश्वर्यम् + अतुलम्

    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m joined to the vowel of अतुलम्; the m of अतुलम् becomes anusvāra before प्राप्स्यसे.

  • परमैश्वर्य paramaiśvarya → परम + ऐश्वर्य

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    a + ai → ai.

  • एतद् बुद्ध्या etad buddhyā → एतत् + बुद्ध्या

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before the voiced b. The Gita Press writes the d with a virāma and a space.

  • मत्परिग्रहात् matparigrahāt → मद् + परिग्रहात्

    consonant sandhi inside a compound (devoicing) · खरि च (8.4.55)

    The pronoun stem मद् becomes मत् before the unvoiced p.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • परमैश्वर्यम् paramaiśvaryam

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    परम + ऐश्वर्य
    Vigraha
    परमम् ऐश्वर्यम् paramam aiśvaryam
    Literally
    “supreme lordship”
    In the verse
    the highest wealth and power there is

  • अतुलम् atulam

    nañ bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अ (नञ्) + तुला
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते तुला यस्य तत् na vidyate tulā yasya tat
    Literally
    “having no balance (to be weighed against)”
    In the verse
    beyond comparison

  • मत्परिग्रहात् matparigrahāt

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (the genitive is subjective: ‘my taking (you)’)

    Members
    मद् + परिग्रह
    Vigraha
    मम परिग्रहः, तस्मात् mama parigrahaḥ, tasmāt
    Literally
    “from my taking”
    In the verse
    through becoming my wife

  • मत्परिग्रहताम् matparigrahatām

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the abstract suffix -ता

    Members
    मद् + परिग्रह (+ tal)
    Vigraha
    मम परिग्रहः मत्परिग्रहः; तस्य भावः मत्परिग्रहता, ताम् mama parigrahaḥ matparigrahaḥ; tasya bhāvaḥ matparigrahatā, tām
    Literally
    “the state of being my possession, my wife”
    In the verse
    become my consort
    Alternative
    परिग्रह means both ‘taking, acceptance (in marriage)’ and ‘what is taken: wife, possession’. In मत्परिग्रहात् the act is meant, in मत्परिग्रहताम् the status; the same word was used of possessions in 5.98 (भ्रातुस्तव परिग्रहे).
Morphology & anvaya

  • प्राप्स्यसे prāpsyase

    Future of प्र + √आप् (आप् + स्य → आप्स्य-), with the ātmanepada ending -से where classical usage has the parasmaipada प्राप्स्यसि. Such voice changes are frequent in the epic and Purāṇic language.

  • मत्परिग्रहताम् matparigrahatām

    The abstract suffix -ता (tal) makes a feminine noun of state from the compound: ‘my-consort-ness’. With a verb of motion (व्रज) the accusative names the condition one passes into: ‘go to the state of …’, i.e. ‘become …’.

  • समालोच्य samālocya

    Absolutive of the tenth-class root √लोच् with two prefixes: the causative -ay- is dropped before -य (lyap). ‘Having weighed (it) well’.

Anvaya · prose order

मत्परिग्रहात् (त्वम्) अतुलं परमैश्वर्यं प्राप्स्यसे। एतत् बुद्ध्या समालोच्य मत्परिग्रहतां व्रज।

matparigrahāt (tvam) atulaṃ paramaiśvaryaṃ prāpsyase | etat buddhyā samālocya matparigrahatāṃ vraja |

A promise in the future (प्राप्स्यसे), then an imperative (व्रज) preceded by an absolutive (समालोच्य) with its object एतत्.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The message ends with an inducement and a command: marriage to Śumbha will give the Devī matchless power; she should think it over and consent.

Traditional interpretation

The offer reverses the truth the text has already stated: the gods’ hymn has just praised her as the power present in all beings (5.14–80), and the narrator will call her the one by whom the world is upheld (5.116). Śumbha offers her what is already hers.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: the appeal to ‘consider it with your intelligence’ invites calculation. The Devī will answer not with a calculation but with a vow made long before (5.119–120).

Verse 5.115

Speaker
‹›

ऋषिरुवाच॥⁠११५⁠॥

ṛṣiruvāca ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ऋषिः · उवाच

ṛṣiḥ · uvāca

Word by word

  1. ऋषिःṛṣiḥthe seer (Medhas)m. nom. sg. of ऋषि (i-stem); subject of उवाच
  2. उवाचuvācasaid√वच् (adādi 2P ‘to speak’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

The seer said:

In readable EnglishThe Seer said:

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • ऋषिरुवाच ṛṣiruvāca → ऋषिः + उवाच

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After a vowel other than a/ā, the visarga (रु) appears as r before a vowel.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

No compound in this verse needs analysis.

Morphology & anvaya

  • उवाच uvāca

    Perfect of √वच्, as in 1.1: the narrative past.

Anvaya · prose order

ऋषिः उवाच।

ṛṣiḥ uvāca |

A speaker label.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Medhas, the seer, takes up the narration again after the messenger’s speech (5.106–114) and tells King Suratha how the Devī replied.

Verse 5.116

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

इत्युक्ता सा तदा देवी गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता जगौ।दुर्गा भगवती भद्रा ययेदं धार्यते जगत्॥⁠११६⁠॥

ityuktā sā tadā devī gambhīrāntaḥsmitā jagau |durgā bhagavatī bhadrā yayedaṃ dhāryate jagat ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

इति · उक्ता · सा · तदा · देवी · गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता · जगौदुर्गा · भगवती · भद्रा · यया · इदम् · धार्यते · जगत्

iti · uktā · sā · tadā · devī · gambhīrāntaḥsmitā · jagaudurgā · bhagavatī · bhadrā · yayā · idam · dhāryate · jagat

Word by word

  1. इतिitithusindeclinable (quotative particle: closes the reported speech)
  2. उक्ताuktāaddressed, spoken topast passive participle (kta) of √वच्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies सा देवी
  3. साsāshe, thatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies देवी
  4. तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
  5. देवीdevīthe Goddessf. nom. sg. of देवी; subject of जगौ
  6. गम्भीरान्तःस्मिताgambhīrāntaḥsmitāgrave, and smiling withinf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies देवी
  7. जगौjagauspoke; lit. ‘sang’√गै (bhvādi 1P ‘to sing’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  8. दुर्गाdurgāDurgāf. nom. sg. of दुर्गा; in apposition to देवी
  9. भगवतीbhagavatīthe Blessed Onef. nom. sg. of भगवती (भगवत् + ṅīp); in apposition to देवी
  10. भद्राbhadrāthe auspiciousf. nom. sg. of भद्रा; in apposition to देवी
  11. ययाyayāby whomrelative pronoun यद्, f. instr. sg.; agent of धार्यते
  12. इदम्idamthispronoun इदम्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies जगत्
  13. धार्यतेdhāryateis upheld, is sustained√धृ (bhvādi 1U ‘to hold’), causative stem धारय-, karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
  14. जगत्jagatworldn. nom. sg. of जगत्; subject of the passive

Translation

Thus addressed, the Devī then, grave and smiling within, spoke — Durgā, the Blessed, the Auspicious, by whom this world is upheld.

In readable EnglishSo addressed, the Devī — Durgā, the Blessed, the Auspicious, by whom this world is upheld — grave of face yet smiling inwardly, gave her answer.

The appositive names are brought forward next to देवी.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • इत्युक्ता ityuktā → इति + उक्ता

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    i before u becomes y.

  • गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता gambhīrāntaḥsmitā → गम्भीर + अन्तः + स्मिता

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha); visarga kept · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā. The visarga of अन्तः stays before the sibilant s inside the compound.

  • ययेदं yayedaṃ → यया + इदम्

    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    ā + i → e; the final m of इदम् becomes anusvāra before धार्यते (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता gambhīrāntaḥsmitā

    karmadhāraya (two attributes of one subject); inside it अन्तःस्मिता = अन्तः स्मिता ‘smiling within’ (the adverb अन्तः with the participle स्मिता)

    Members
    गम्भीरा + अन्तःस्मिता
    Vigraha
    गम्भीरा च सा अन्तःस्मिता च gambhīrā ca sā antaḥsmitā ca
    Literally
    “grave and inwardly smiling”
    In the verse
    outwardly serious, with a smile kept inside
    Alternative
    Also read as a bahuvrīhi, गम्भीरम् अन्तः स्मितं यस्याः सा ‘she whose inner smile was deep’. Both keep the contrast of a grave face and a hidden smile; the karmadhāraya keeps the simpler parts.
Morphology & anvaya

  • जगौ jagau

    Perfect of √गै ‘sing’: reduplicated ज- (g → j) + गौ. Used here, as often in the Purāṇas, simply for ‘spoke, declared’.

  • धार्यते dhāryate

    Passive of the causative धारय- (√धृ ‘to hold’): the causative -ay- is dropped before the passive -ya-: धार्-य-ते, ‘is upheld’.

  • उक्ता uktā

    Past participle of √वच् with samprasāraṇa (va → u): उक्त. In the passive sense of a verb of speaking it means ‘the one spoken to’.

Anvaya · prose order

इति उक्ता सा देवी, दुर्गा भगवती भद्रा, यया इदं जगत् धार्यते, तदा गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता जगौ।

iti uktā sā devī, durgā bhagavatī bhadrā, yayā idaṃ jagat dhāryate, tadā gambhīrāntaḥsmitā jagau |

The subject देवी has three appositive names and a relative clause (यया … धार्यते); the main verb is जगौ with the predicate adjective गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The narrator introduces the Devī’s reply. The contrast is marked: she is grave, but smiles within. The second line steps out of the scene to remind the hearer who is being asked to marry Śumbha — the one by whom the world is upheld.

Traditional interpretation

The hidden smile is read as the key to her reply: what she says will be courteous and literally true, while its meaning is the opposite of surrender. The name Durgā, ‘hard to approach’, is apt at the very moment an asura proposes to take her.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has two of the names given here: 190, दुर्गा, and 279, भगवती. The coincidence of names reflects a shared Śākta vocabulary; the two texts are separate works.

  • उवाच uvāca

    Perfect of √वच्, as in 1.1.

देवी उवाच।

devī uvāca |
