Direct textual meaning

The Devī answers Śumbha’s messenger Sugrīva, who has delivered his master’s proposal (5.108–114). Her reply runs to 5.121.

Verse 5.118

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

सत्यमुक्तं त्वया नात्र मिथ्या किञ्चित्त्वयोदितम्।त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः॥⁠११८⁠॥

satyamuktaṃ tvayā nātra mithyā kiñcittvayoditam |trailokyādhipatiḥ śumbho niśumbhaścāpi tādṛśaḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सत्यम् · उक्तम् · त्वया · न · अत्र · मिथ्या · किञ्चित् · त्वया · उदितम्त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः · शुम्भः · निशुम्भः · च · अपि · तादृशः

satyam · uktam · tvayā · na · atra · mithyā · kiñcit · tvayā · uditamtrailokyādhipatiḥ · śumbhaḥ · niśumbhaḥ · ca · api · tādṛśaḥ

Word by word

  1. सत्यम्satyamtruth, the truthn. nom. sg. of सत्य; predicate of उक्तम् (‘what was said is true’)
  2. उक्तम्uktam(has been) saidpast passive participle (kta) of √वच्, n. nom. sg.
  3. त्वयाtvayāby yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of उक्तम्
  4. नnanotindeclinable (negative particle)
  5. अत्रatrahere, in thisindeclinable (adverb of place)
  6. मिथ्याmithyāfalse, falselyindeclinable (used as a predicate adjective: ‘false’)
  7. किञ्चित्kiñcitanything; with न, ‘nothing at all’indefinite pronoun (किम् + चित्), n. nom. sg.; subject of उदितम्
  8. त्वयाtvayāby yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of उदितम्
  9. उदितम्uditamspokenpast passive participle (kta) of √वद् (bhvādi 1P ‘to speak’), n. nom. sg.
  10. त्रैलोक्याधिपतिःtrailokyādhipatiḥthe overlord of the three worldsm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of शुम्भः
  11. शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject
  12. निशुम्भःniśumbhaḥNiśumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject
  13. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  14. अपिapialsoindeclinable (particle)
  15. तादृशःtādṛśaḥsuch, of the same kindpronominal adjective तादृश (तद् + दृश), m. nom. sg.; predicate of निशुम्भः

Translation

Truth has been spoken by you; nothing false at all has been uttered by you here. Śumbha is the overlord of the three worlds, and Niśumbha too is such.

In readable EnglishWhat you say is true; you have spoken no falsehood here. Śumbha is lord of the three worlds, and Niśumbha is his equal.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • नात्र nātra → न + अत्र

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

  • किञ्चित्त्वयोदितम् kiñcittvayoditam → किञ्चित् + त्वया + उदितम्

    no change; vowel sandhi (guṇa) · खरि च (8.4.55); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    The t of किञ्चित् stays t before the t of त्वया (written as a double t); then ā + u → o.

  • शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चापि śumbho niśumbhaścāpi → शुम्भः + निशुम्भः + च + अपि

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    -aḥ before the voiced n becomes -o; visarga before c becomes ś; a + a → ā.

  • त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः trailokyādhipatiḥ → त्रैलोक्य + अधिपतिः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः trailokyādhipatiḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    त्रैलोक्य + अधिपति
    Vigraha
    त्रैलोक्यस्य अधिपतिः trailokyasya adhipatiḥ
    Literally
    “overlord of the three worlds”
    In the verse
    ruler of the whole universe
Morphology & anvaya

  • उदितम् uditam

    Past participle of √वद् with samprasāraṇa (va → u) and the connecting i: उद्-इत. Synonymous with उक्तम् in the first half; the line says the same thing twice, positively and negatively.

  • तादृशः tādṛśaḥ

    तद् + दृश ‘looking like that’: the pronoun stem lengthens its vowel (ता-) before दृश. ‘Such’, i.e. of the same rank as Śumbha.

Anvaya · prose order

त्वया सत्यम् उक्तम्; अत्र त्वया किञ्चित् मिथ्या न उदितम्। शुम्भः त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः, निशुम्भः च अपि तादृशः (अस्ति)।

tvayā satyam uktam; atra tvayā kiñcit mithyā na uditam | śumbhaḥ trailokyādhipatiḥ, niśumbhaḥ ca api tādṛśaḥ (asti) |

Two passive statements with the participles उक्तम् and उदितम् (verb ‘is’ understood), then two nominal sentences.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Devī begins by granting everything the messenger has said about the brothers’ power. She does not dispute their rank; her objection will lie elsewhere.

Traditional interpretation

Read with the smile of 5.116, the concession is ironic: she accepts the facts in order to bring in a condition the facts cannot meet. Note that 5.128, as the Gita Press prints it, does not repeat the phrase निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः, though other witnesses read it there too (see 5.128).

Verse 5.119

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

किं त्वत्र यत्प्रतिज्ञातं मिथ्या तत्क्रियते कथम्।श्रूयतामल्पबुद्धित्वात्प्रतिज्ञा या कृता पुरा॥⁠११९⁠॥

kiṃ tvatra yatpratijñātaṃ mithyā tatkriyate katham |śrūyatāmalpabuddhitvātpratijñā yā kṛtā purā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

किम् · तु · अत्र · यत् · प्रतिज्ञातम् · मिथ्या · तत् · क्रियते · कथम्श्रूयताम् · अल्पबुद्धित्वात् · प्रतिज्ञा · या · कृता · पुरा

kim · tu · atra · yat · pratijñātam · mithyā · tat · kriyate · kathamśrūyatām · alpabuddhitvāt · pratijñā · yā · kṛtā · purā

Word by word

  1. किम्kimbut (with तु)indeclinable (किम्; with तु forms the adversative ‘but’)
  2. तुtubutindeclinable (adversative particle)
  3. अत्रatrahere, in this matterindeclinable (adverb of place)
  4. यत्yatwhatrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. sg.; correlative तत्
  5. प्रतिज्ञातम्pratijñātamhas been promised, was vowedpast passive participle (kta) of प्रति + √ज्ञा (kryādi 9 ‘to know’; with प्रति ‘to promise’), n. nom. sg.
  6. मिथ्याmithyāfalseindeclinable (predicate: ‘made false’)
  7. तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. nom. sg.; correlative of यत्; subject of क्रियते
  8. क्रियतेkriyatecan be made, is made√कृ (tanādi 8U ‘to do, make’), karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
  9. कथम्kathamhow?indeclinable (interrogative adverb)
  10. श्रूयताम्śrūyatāmlet it be heard, hear√श्रु (svādi 5 ‘to hear’), karmaṇi prayoga, loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
  11. अल्पबुद्धित्वात्alpabuddhitvātout of little understandingn. abl. sg. of अल्पबुद्धित्व (compound + abstract suffix tva); ablative of cause
  12. प्रतिज्ञाpratijñāthe vow, the promisef. nom. sg. of प्रतिज्ञा; subject of the passive श्रूयताम्
  13. याyāwhichrelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies प्रतिज्ञा
  14. कृताkṛtāwas madepast passive participle (kta) of √कृ, f. nom. sg.
  15. पुराpurāformerly, long agoindeclinable (adverb of time)

Translation

But here — what has been vowed, how is that to be made false? Let the vow be heard which was made formerly out of little understanding.

In readable EnglishBut in this matter there is a vow I made, and how could I break it? Hear the promise I once made, foolishly.

The passive constructions are turned active (‘I made’, ‘could I break’); the agent ‘I’ is implied by the context.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • किं त्वत्र kiṃ tvatra → किम् + तु + अत्र

    anusvāra sandhi; vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    Final m before t becomes anusvāra; u before a becomes v.

  • यत्प्रतिज्ञातं yatpratijñātaṃ → यत् + प्रतिज्ञातम्

    no change · खरि च (8.4.55)

    Final t stays unvoiced before the unvoiced p; likewise तत्क्रियते and अल्पबुद्धित्वात्प्रतिज्ञा.

  • श्रूयतामल्पबुद्धित्वात् śrūyatāmalpabuddhitvāt → श्रूयताम् + अल्पबुद्धित्वात्

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    The m is written together with the following vowel.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अल्पबुद्धित्वात् alpabuddhitvāt

    bahuvrīhi (अल्पबुद्धि) with the abstract suffix -त्व

    Members
    अल्प + बुद्धि (+ tva)
    Vigraha
    अल्पा बुद्धिः यस्याः सा अल्पबुद्धिः; तस्याः भावः अल्पबुद्धित्वम्, तस्मात् alpā buddhiḥ yasyāḥ sā alpabuddhiḥ; tasyāḥ bhāvaḥ alpabuddhitvam, tasmāt
    Literally
    “from the state of being one whose understanding is small”
    In the verse
    out of thoughtlessness, foolishly
Morphology & anvaya

  • क्रियते kriyate

    Passive present of √कृ: the root becomes क्रि- before the passive -य- (क्रियते). With मिथ्या: ‘is made false’; with कथम्, ‘how can it be made false?’.

  • श्रूयताम् śrūyatām

    Passive imperative of √श्रु (the root vowel lengthened before -य-: श्रूय-): ‘let it be heard’, the polite form of ‘hear’.

  • अल्पबुद्धित्वात् alpabuddhitvāt

    Ablative of cause with the abstract -त्व: ‘because of being of small understanding’. Feigned modesty: compare अनालोचिता ‘unconsidered’ in 5.128.

Anvaya · prose order

किं तु अत्र यत् प्रतिज्ञातं, तत् कथं मिथ्या क्रियते? अल्पबुद्धित्वात् (मया) पुरा या प्रतिज्ञा कृता, (सा) श्रूयताम्।

kiṃ tu atra yat pratijñātaṃ, tat kathaṃ mithyā kriyate? alpabuddhitvāt (mayā) purā yā pratijñā kṛtā, (sā) śrūyatām |

A rhetorical question built on a relative pair (यत् … तत्), then a passive imperative whose subject प्रतिज्ञा has a relative clause (या कृता पुरा).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Devī introduces an obstacle: a vow she made long ago. She cannot make it false, and she calls it the product of her ‘little understanding’.

Traditional interpretation

The self-deprecation is read as irony matching her inward smile (5.116): the Goddess who is praised as बुद्धि itself (5.20) claims to have acted from small understanding. The vow is not a mistake but the trap into which the asuras will walk.

Verse 5.120

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

यो मां जयति संग्रामे यो मे दर्पं व्यपोहति।यो मे प्रतिबलो लोके स मे भर्ता भविष्यति॥⁠१२०⁠॥

yo māṃ jayati saṃgrāme yo me darpaṃ vyapohati |yo me pratibalo loke sa me bhartā bhaviṣyati ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यः · माम् · जयति · संग्रामे · यः · मे · दर्पम् · व्यपोहतियः · मे · प्रतिबलः · लोके · सः · मे · भर्ता · भविष्यति

yaḥ · mām · jayati · saṃgrāme · yaḥ · me · darpam · vyapohatiyaḥ · me · pratibalaḥ · loke · saḥ · me · bhartā · bhaviṣyati

Word by word

  1. यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of जयति
  2. माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of जयति
  3. जयतिjayaticonquers, defeats√जि (bhvādi 1P ‘to conquer’), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  4. संग्रामेsaṃgrāmein battlem. loc. sg. of संग्राम
  5. यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of व्यपोहति
  6. मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with दर्पम्
  7. दर्पम्darpampridem. acc. sg. of दर्प; object of व्यपोहति
  8. व्यपोहतिvyapohatiremoves, drives awayवि + अप + √ऊह् (bhvādi 1 ‘to push, remove’; here parasmaipada), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
  9. यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject (verb ‘is’ understood)
  10. मेmeto me, myfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with प्रतिबलः ‘a match for me’
  11. प्रतिबलःpratibalaḥequal in strength, a matchm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate
  12. लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
  13. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; correlative of the three यः; subject of भविष्यति
  14. मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with भर्ता
  15. भर्ताbhartāhusbandm. nom. sg. of भर्तृ (√भृ + tṛc, ‘supporter’); predicate of सः
  16. भविष्यतिbhaviṣyatiwill be√भू (bhvādi 1P), lṛṭ (simple future), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

He who conquers me in battle, he who removes my pride, he who is my match in strength in the world — he will be my husband.

In readable English“He who conquers me in battle, who humbles my pride, who is my equal in strength in all the world — he shall be my husband.”

Set in quotation marks as the words of the vow announced in 5.119.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • यो मां yo māṃ → यः + माम्

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before a voiced consonant becomes -o; the same in यो मे (twice) and प्रतिबलो लोके.

  • दर्पं व्यपोहति darpaṃ vyapohati → दर्पम् + व्यपोहति

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant is written as anusvāra.

  • व्यपोहति vyapohati → वि + अप + ऊहति

    vowel sandhi between prefixes and root (yaṇ, guṇa) · इको यणचि (6.1.77); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    i before a becomes y (व्यप-); a + ū → o (अपोह-).

  • स मे sa me → सः + मे

    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)

    The visarga of सः is dropped before a consonant.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • प्रतिबलः pratibalaḥ

    bahuvrīhi (prādi-type, with the prefix प्रति ‘matching’)

    Members
    प्रति + बल
    Vigraha
    प्रति (समानं) बलं यस्य सः prati (samānaṃ) balaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “one whose strength is counter (to mine)”
    In the verse
    one equal to me in strength
    Alternative
    Also read as a prādi tatpuruṣa, प्रतिगतं बलम् ‘a counter-force’, used of a person; the bahuvrīhi suits the masculine agreement.
Morphology & anvaya

  • जयति jayati

    Present tense in a general relative clause with future sense: ‘whoever conquers (= shall conquer) me’. The main clause has the future भविष्यति.

  • भर्ता bhartā

    Nominative singular of the agent noun भर्तृ (√भृ ‘to bear, support’ + -तृ): literally ‘supporter, maintainer’, the ordinary word for ‘husband’.

  • व्यपोहति vyapohati

    वि + अप + √ऊह् ‘push away, dispel’. The root is listed as ātmanepada; the parasmaipada व्यपोहति is the usual epic form.

Anvaya · prose order

यः मां संग्रामे जयति, यः मे दर्पं व्यपोहति, यः लोके मे प्रतिबलः (अस्ति), सः मे भर्ता भविष्यति।

yaḥ māṃ saṃgrāme jayati, yaḥ me darpaṃ vyapohati, yaḥ loke me pratibalaḥ (asti), saḥ me bhartā bhaviṣyati |

Three relative clauses, each opened by यः, all taken up by the single correlative सः of the main clause.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The vow itself, in three conditions that come to one: the husband must defeat her in battle, break her pride, and match her strength. The repetition of यः … यः … यः gives it the form of a formal pledge.

Traditional interpretation

Since the text has already shown her to be the power of all the gods together (2.9–30) and the power present in all beings (5.14–80), no one can meet the condition. The tradition reads the vow as a statement of her sovereignty: the Goddess is not won as a possession, and her only ‘husband’ would be her equal.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: the Devī answers a claim of ownership with a test of strength. She does not argue with Śumbha’s logic of jewels; she changes the terms on which it can be applied.

Verse 5.121

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

तदागच्छतु शुम्भोऽत्र निशुम्भो वा महासुरः।मां जित्वा किं चिरेणात्र पाणिं गृह्णातु मे लघु॥⁠१२१⁠॥

tadāgacchatu śumbho'tra niśumbho vā mahāsuraḥ |māṃ jitvā kiṃ cireṇātra pāṇiṃ gṛhṇātu me laghu ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

तत् · आगच्छतु · शुम्भः · अत्र · निशुम्भः · वा · महासुरःमाम् · जित्वा · किम् · चिरेण · अत्र · पाणिम् · गृह्णातु · मे · लघु

tat · āgacchatu · śumbhaḥ · atra · niśumbhaḥ · vā · mahāsuraḥmām · jitvā · kim · cireṇa · atra · pāṇim · gṛhṇātu · me · laghu

Word by word

  1. तत्tatso, thereforepronoun तद्, n. acc. sg., used as an indeclinable (‘that being so’)
  2. आगच्छतुāgacchatulet (him) comeआ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  3. शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject
  4. अत्रatrahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  5. निशुम्भःniśumbhaḥNiśumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; alternative subject
  6. वाvāorindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
  7. महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies निशुम्भः
  8. माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of जित्वा
  9. जित्वाjitvāhaving conqueredabsolutive (ktvā) of √जि (bhvādi 1P ‘to conquer’)
  10. किम्kimwhat (use)?, why?interrogative pronoun किम्, n. nom. sg., used adverbially
  11. चिरेणcireṇawith delayn. instr. sg. of चिर; with किम् ‘what is the use of delay?’
  12. अत्रatrahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
  13. पाणिम्pāṇimhandm. acc. sg. of पाणि (i-stem); object of गृह्णातु
  14. गृह्णातुgṛhṇātulet (him) take√ग्रह् (kryādi 9P ‘to seize’, stem गृह्णा-), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  15. मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with पाणिम्
  16. लघुlaghuquicklyn. acc. sg. of लघु, used adverbially

Translation

So let Śumbha come here, or Niśumbha the great asura; having conquered me — what (use) is delay? — let him take my hand here quickly.

In readable EnglishSo let Śumbha come here, or Niśumbha, the mighty asura. Let him conquer me and, without delay, take my hand.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तदागच्छतु tadāgacchatu → तत् + आगच्छतु

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before a vowel.

  • शुम्भोऽत्र śumbho'tra → शुम्भः + अत्र

    visarga sandhi with pūrvarūpa · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), अतो रोरप्लुतादप्लुते (6.1.113), आद्गुणः (6.1.87), एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)

    -aḥ before a becomes -o, and the following a is absorbed (avagraha).

  • निशुम्भो वा niśumbho vā → निशुम्भः + वा

    visarga sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    -aḥ before the voiced v becomes -o.

  • चिरेणात्र cireṇātra → चिरेण + अत्र

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • महासुरः mahāsuraḥ

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    महत् + असुर
    Vigraha
    महान् चासौ असुरः च mahān cāsau asuraḥ ca
    Literally
    “a great asura”
    In the verse
    the mighty demon (महत् becomes महा- before a noun in the same case)
Morphology & anvaya

  • गृह्णातु gṛhṇātu

    Imperative of √ग्रह्, ninth class, with samprasāraṇa (ग्रह् → गृह्) before -णा-: गृह्णातु. पाणिं गृह्णातु ‘let him take (my) hand’ is the idiom for marriage (पाणिग्रहण).

  • जित्वा jitvā

    Absolutive of √जि without a prefix: -त्वा. It sets the condition before the marriage: first conquer, then take the hand.

  • किम् kim

    With the instrumental, किम् asks ‘what (is gained) by …?’: किं चिरेण ‘why delay?’.

Anvaya · prose order

तत् शुम्भः महासुरः निशुम्भः वा अत्र आगच्छतु। मां जित्वा मे पाणिम् अत्र लघु गृह्णातु। चिरेण किम्?

tat śumbhaḥ mahāsuraḥ niśumbhaḥ vā atra āgacchatu | māṃ jitvā me pāṇim atra laghu gṛhṇātu | cireṇa kim?

Two third-person imperatives (आगच्छतु, गृह्णातु), the second preceded by the absolutive जित्वा; the rhetorical किं चिरेण stands between them.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Devī applies the vow to the proposal. Either brother may come, as Śumbha offered (5.113), but first he must defeat her; then let him marry her at once.

Traditional interpretation

The urgency (किं चिरेण, लघु) mirrors the messenger’s demand that she come quickly (5.103, लघु). The invitation is a challenge: the next chapters bring first the asura generals and finally the two brothers to fight her.

A speaker label.

Verse 5.123

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

अवलिप्तासि मैवं त्वं देवि ब्रूहि ममाग्रतः।त्रैलोक्ये कः पुमांस्तिष्ठेदग्रे शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः॥⁠१२३⁠॥

avaliptāsi maivaṃ tvaṃ devi brūhi mamāgrataḥ |trailokye kaḥ pumāṃstiṣṭhedagre śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

अवलिप्ता · असि · मा · एवम् · त्वम् · देवि · ब्रूहि · मम · अग्रतःत्रैलोक्ये · कः · पुमान् · तिष्ठेत् · अग्रे · शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः

avaliptā · asi · mā · evam · tvam · devi · brūhi · mama · agrataḥtrailokye · kaḥ · pumān · tiṣṭhet · agre · śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

Word by word

  1. अवलिप्ताavaliptāarrogant; lit. ‘smeared (with pride)’past passive participle (kta) of अव + √लिप् (tudādi 6U ‘to smear’), f. nom. sg.; predicate
  2. असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi 2P ‘to be’), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  3. माmādo notindeclinable (prohibitive particle)
  4. एवम्evamthus, like thisindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  5. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of ब्रूहि
  6. देविdeviO Devī, O ladyf. voc. sg. of देवी
  7. ब्रूहिbrūhispeak√ब्रू (adādi 2U ‘to speak’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  8. ममmamaof mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with अग्रतः
  9. अग्रतःagrataḥin front of, in the presence ofindeclinable (अग्र + tasil), governing the genitive मम
  10. त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य
  11. कःkaḥwhat, whichinterrogative pronoun किम्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies पुमान्
  12. पुमान्pumānmanm. nom. sg. of पुंस्; subject of तिष्ठेत्
  13. तिष्ठेत्tiṣṭhetcould stand√स्था (bhvādi 1P ‘to stand’, stem तिष्ठ-), vidhiliṅ (optative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  14. अग्रेagrein front of, beforen. loc. sg. of अग्र, used as a postposition with the genitive
  15. शुम्भनिशुम्भयोःśumbhaniśumbhayoḥof Śumbha and Niśumbham. gen. du.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on अग्रे

Translation

You are arrogant, O Devī; do not speak thus in front of me. In the three worlds what man could stand before Śumbha and Niśumbha?

In readable EnglishYou are proud, O Devī. Do not speak so in my presence. What man in the three worlds could stand before Śumbha and Niśumbha?

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • अवलिप्तासि avaliptāsi → अवलिप्ता + असि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • मैवं maivaṃ → मा + एवम्

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    ā + e → ai; the final m becomes anusvāra before त्वम् (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

  • पुमांस्तिष्ठेदग्रे pumāṃstiṣṭhedagre → पुमान् + तिष्ठेत् + अग्रे

    consonant sandhi (n before t); voicing · नश्छव्यप्रशान् (8.3.7); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final n before t becomes ṃs (पुमांस्तिष्ठेत्); the t of तिष्ठेत् becomes d before the vowel of अग्रे.

  • ममाग्रतः mamāgrataḥ → मम + अग्रतः

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

    itaretara dvandva (dual)

    Members
    शुम्भ + निशुम्भ
    Vigraha
    शुम्भः च निशुम्भः च, तयोः śumbhaḥ ca niśumbhaḥ ca, tayoḥ
    Literally
    “of Śumbha and Niśumbha”
    In the verse
    of the two brothers
Morphology & anvaya

  • ब्रूहि brūhi

    Imperative of √ब्रू with the ending -हि. After मा, Pāṇinian Sanskrit uses the aorist without augment (मा वोचः); मा with the imperative is the looser usage of the epics and Purāṇas, with the same prohibitive sense.

  • तिष्ठेत् tiṣṭhet

    Optative of √स्था (present stem तिष्ठ-): ‘could, would stand’. In a rhetorical question it means ‘no one could’.

  • पुमान् pumān

    Nominative singular of the irregular stem पुंस् ‘man’: पुमान्, पुमांसौ, पुमांसः.

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, त्वम् अवलिप्ता असि; मम अग्रतः एवं मा ब्रूहि। त्रैलोक्ये कः पुमान् शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः अग्रे तिष्ठेत्?

devi, tvam avaliptā asi; mama agrataḥ evaṃ mā brūhi | trailokye kaḥ pumān śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ agre tiṣṭhet?

A statement, a prohibition (मा … ब्रूहि), and a rhetorical question with the optative.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The messenger takes the vow as arrogance (अवलिप्ता) and rejects it: no man in the three worlds can face the two brothers, so a contest is absurd.

Traditional interpretation

The word दर्प ‘pride’ of the vow (5.120) is answered with अवलिप्ता ‘puffed up’. The irony runs the other way in the narrative: it is the asuras’ pride that will be removed, in the battles of chapters 6–10. Śumbha himself will make the same charge at the end, बलावलेपात् ‘from pride in strength’ (10.3).

Verse 5.124

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

अन्येषामपि दैत्यानां सर्वे देवा न वै युधि।तिष्ठन्ति सम्मुखे देवि किं पुनः स्त्री त्वमेकिका॥⁠१२४⁠॥

anyeṣāmapi daityānāṃ sarve devā na vai yudhi |tiṣṭhanti sammukhe devi kiṃ punaḥ strī tvamekikā ||

अन्येषाम् · अपि · दैत्यानाम् · सर्वे · देवाः · न · वै · युधितिष्ठन्ति · सम्मुखे · देवि · किम् · पुनः · स्त्री · त्वम् · एकिका

anyeṣām · api · daityānām · sarve · devāḥ · na · vai · yudhitiṣṭhanti · sammukhe · devi · kim · punaḥ · strī · tvam · ekikā

Word by word

  1. अन्येषाम्anyeṣāmof otherpronominal adjective अन्य, m. gen. pl.; qualifies दैत्यानाम्
  2. अपिapievenindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  3. दैत्यानाम्daityānāmDaityasm. gen. pl. of दैत्य; depends on सम्मुखे
  4. सर्वेsarveallpronominal adjective सर्व, m. nom. pl.; qualifies देवाः
  5. देवाःdevāḥthe godsm. nom. pl. of देव; subject of तिष्ठन्ति
  6. नnanotindeclinable (negative particle)
  7. वैvaiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  8. युधिyudhiin battlef. loc. sg. of युध् (consonant stem)
  9. तिष्ठन्तिtiṣṭhantistand√स्था (bhvādi 1P, stem तिष्ठ-), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  10. सम्मुखेsammukheface to face, in frontn. loc. sg. of सम्मुख, used as a postposition with the genitive दैत्यानाम्
  11. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  12. किम्kimhow much (less)indeclinable (interrogative किम्); with पुनः ‘how much less’
  13. पुनःpunaḥagain; with किम् ‘how much less’indeclinable (adverb)
  14. स्त्रीstrīa womanf. nom. sg. of स्त्री; in apposition to त्वम्
  15. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.
  16. एकिकाekikāall alone, by yourselff. nom. sg. of एकिका (एक + taddhita -क, feminine एकिका); qualifies त्वम्

Translation

All the gods do not indeed stand in battle face to face even with other Daityas, O Devī — how much less you, a woman, alone?

In readable EnglishNot all the gods together can stand in battle even before the lesser Daityas, O Devī — how much less you, a woman, alone!

अन्येषाम् ‘other’ is rendered ‘lesser’ to bring out the contrast with Śumbha and Niśumbha.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • अन्येषामपि anyeṣāmapi → अन्येषाम् + अपि

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    The m is written together with the following vowel.

  • देवा न devā na → देवाः + न

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -āḥ before a voiced consonant: the visarga is lost, leaving -ā.

  • त्वमेकिका tvamekikā → त्वम् + एकिका

    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel

    The m is written together with the following vowel.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

No compound in this verse needs analysis.

Morphology & anvaya

  • एकिका ekikā

    एक ‘one’ with the suffix -क (here diminishing or contemptuous: ‘a mere lone …’); the feminine turns the a before क to i (एकिका). It mocks the Devī for standing without an army.

  • पुनः punaḥ

    In the idiom किं पुनः, ‘what again …?’ means ‘how much less (or more)’: an argument from the greater to the lesser.

  • युधि yudhi

    Locative singular of the root-noun युध् ‘battle’ (feminine): युध् + इ.

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, सर्वे देवाः अन्येषाम् अपि दैत्यानां सम्मुखे युधि न वै तिष्ठन्ति; एकिका स्त्री त्वं किं पुनः?

devi, sarve devāḥ anyeṣām api daityānāṃ sammukhe yudhi na vai tiṣṭhanti; ekikā strī tvaṃ kiṃ punaḥ?

A negative statement followed by the elliptical rhetorical question किं पुनः (‘how much less [will] you …’), whose verb is understood from तिष्ठन्ति.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The messenger argues from the greater to the lesser: the gods cannot face even the other Daityas; a lone woman cannot face the brothers.

Traditional interpretation

Being alone and a woman is, for the messenger, the decisive weakness. The narrative will turn both against the asuras: Śumbha will taunt her for relying on other goddesses, and she will withdraw them all into herself to fight alone (10.3–6).

Verse 5.125

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

इन्द्राद्याः सकला देवास्तस्थुर्येषां न संयुगे।शुम्भादीनां कथं तेषां स्त्री प्रयास्यसि सम्मुखम्॥⁠१२५⁠॥

indrādyāḥ sakalā devāstasthuryeṣāṃ na saṃyuge |śumbhādīnāṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ strī prayāsyasi sammukham ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

इन्द्राद्याः · सकलाः · देवाः · तस्थुः · येषाम् · न · संयुगेशुम्भादीनाम् · कथम् · तेषाम् · स्त्री · प्रयास्यसि · सम्मुखम्

indrādyāḥ · sakalāḥ · devāḥ · tasthuḥ · yeṣām · na · saṃyugeśumbhādīnām · katham · teṣām · strī · prayāsyasi · sammukham

Word by word

  1. इन्द्राद्याःindrādyāḥheaded by Indram. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies देवाः
  2. सकलाःsakalāḥalladjective सकल, m. nom. pl.; qualifies देवाः
  3. देवाःdevāḥthe godsm. nom. pl. of देव; subject of तस्थुः
  4. तस्थुःtasthuḥstood (their ground)√स्था (bhvādi 1P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
  5. येषाम्yeṣāmbefore whom (lit. ‘of whom’)relative pronoun यद्, m. gen. pl.; with संयुगे (‘in battle with whom’); correlative तेषाम्
  6. नnanotindeclinable (negative particle)
  7. संयुगेsaṃyugein battlen. loc. sg. of संयुग
  8. शुम्भादीनाम्śumbhādīnāmof Śumbha and the othersm. gen. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to तेषाम्
  9. कथम्kathamhow?indeclinable (interrogative adverb)
  10. तेषाम्teṣāmof thempronoun तद्, m. gen. pl.; correlative of येषाम्; depends on सम्मुखम्

Translation

Of whom in battle all the gods headed by Indra did not stand (before) — how will you, a woman, go to the front of those, Śumbha and the rest?

In readable EnglishIndra and all the gods could not hold their ground in battle against Śumbha and his host. How will you, a woman, go out to face them?

The relative construction is turned into two sentences.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • इन्द्राद्याः indrādyāḥ → इन्द्र + आद्याः

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā; likewise शुम्भादीनाम् (शुम्भ + आदीनाम्).

  • देवास्तस्थुर्येषां devāstasthuryeṣāṃ → देवाः + तस्थुः + येषाम्

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34); ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    Visarga before t becomes s (देवास्तस्थुः); after u and before the voiced y, the visarga (रु) appears as r (तस्थुर्येषाम्).

  • सकला देवास् sakalā devās → सकलाः + देवाः

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    -āḥ before a voiced consonant: the visarga is lost, leaving -ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • इन्द्राद्याः indrādyāḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    इन्द्र + आदि
    Vigraha
    इन्द्रः आदिः येषां ते indraḥ ādiḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “those of whom Indra is the first”
    In the verse
    Indra and the other gods

  • शुम्भादीनाम् śumbhādīnām

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    शुम्भ + आदि
    Vigraha
    शुम्भः आदिः येषां ते, तेषाम् śumbhaḥ ādiḥ yeṣāṃ te, teṣām
    Literally
    “of those of whom Śumbha is the first”
    In the verse
    of Śumbha and his followers
Morphology & anvaya

  • तस्थुः tasthuḥ

    Perfect third plural of √स्था: reduplicated त- + स्थ् + -उः, the root vowel lost before the ending. ‘Stood (firm)’, i.e. held their ground.

  • प्रयास्यसि prayāsyasi

    Simple future of प्र + √या (या + स्य): ‘will you go’. With कथम्: ‘how will you (dare to) go?’.

  • स्त्री strī

    Nominative singular of the irregular feminine स्त्री, the subject being ‘you’ implied by the verb: ‘(you,) a woman’.

Anvaya · prose order

येषां (सम्मुखे) संयुगे इन्द्राद्याः सकलाः देवाः न तस्थुः, तेषां शुम्भादीनां सम्मुखं (त्वं) स्त्री कथं प्रयास्यसि?

yeṣāṃ (sammukhe) saṃyuge indrādyāḥ sakalāḥ devāḥ na tasthuḥ, teṣāṃ śumbhādīnāṃ sammukhaṃ (tvaṃ) strī kathaṃ prayāsyasi?

A relative clause (येषां … न तस्थुः) names the asuras; the main clause is a rhetorical question with the future प्रयास्यसि, its subject ‘you’ implied.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The messenger repeats his argument with a fact the hearer knows: Indra and all the gods were defeated by these asuras (5.2–5). How then can a woman go out against them?

Traditional interpretation

The listener has just heard the same defeated gods praise the Devī as the power behind all beings (5.9–82). The messenger’s reasoning measures her against the gods; the text has already placed her above them.

Verse 5.126

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

सा त्वं गच्छ मयैवोक्ता पार्श्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः।केशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवा मा गमिष्यसि॥⁠१२६⁠॥

sā tvaṃ gaccha mayaivoktā pārśvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ |keśākarṣaṇanirdhūtagauravā mā gamiṣyasi ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सा · त्वम् · गच्छ · मया · एव · उक्ता · पार्श्वम् · शुम्भनिशुम्भयोःकेशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवा · मा · गमिष्यसि

sā · tvam · gaccha · mayā · eva · uktā · pārśvam · śumbhaniśumbhayoḥkeśākarṣaṇanirdhūtagauravā · mā · gamiṣyasi

Word by word

  1. साsāthat one, such (as you are)pronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies त्वम्
  2. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of गच्छ
  3. गच्छgacchago√गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  4. मयाmayāby mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of उक्ता
  5. एवevajust, aloneindeclinable (restrictive particle): ‘at my word alone’
  6. उक्ताuktātold, biddenpast passive participle (kta) of √वच्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies त्वम्
  7. पार्श्वम्pārśvamto the side (of)n. acc. sg. of पार्श्व; goal of गच्छ
  8. शुम्भनिशुम्भयोःśumbhaniśumbhayoḥof Śumbha and Niśumbham. gen. du.; compound (see Samāsa, 5.123); depends on पार्श्वम्
  9. केशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवाkeśākarṣaṇanirdhūtagauravāwith your dignity destroyed by being dragged by the hairf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies the subject of गमिष्यसि
  10. माmālest, do notindeclinable (prohibitive particle)
  11. गमिष्यसिgamiṣyasiyou go, you will go√गम् (bhvādi 1P), lṛṭ (simple future), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

So go, bidden by me alone, to the side of Śumbha and Niśumbha; do not go (later) with your dignity shaken off by the dragging of your hair.

In readable EnglishGo, then, to Śumbha and Niśumbha now, at my word alone — lest you go dragged there by the hair, stripped of your honour.

‘Now’ and ‘later’ (lit.) and ‘lest’ (tr) bring out the contrast of the two verbs; they are implied, not stated.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • मयैवोक्ता mayaivoktā → मया + एव + उक्ता

    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi, guṇa) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    ā + e → ai (मयैव); a + u → o (एवोक्ता).

  • पार्श्वं शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः pārśvaṃ śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ → पार्श्वम् + शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant is written as anusvāra.

  • केशाकर्षण keśākarṣaṇa → केश + आकर्षण

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • केशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवा keśākarṣaṇanirdhūtagauravā

    bahuvrīhi containing a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (केशाकर्षणनिर्धूत) and a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (केशाकर्षण)

    Members
    केशाकर्षण + निर्धूत + गौरव
    Vigraha
    केशानाम् आकर्षणम् केशाकर्षणम्; केशाकर्षणेन निर्धूतं गौरवं यस्याः सा keśānām ākarṣaṇam keśākarṣaṇam; keśākarṣaṇena nirdhūtaṃ gauravaṃ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she whose dignity has been shaken off by the dragging of the hair”
    In the verse
    dragged by the hair and stripped of all honour
Morphology & anvaya

  • गमिष्यसि gamiṣyasi

    Future of √गम् (गम् + इष्य). With मा it expresses a warning: ‘do not (end up) going …’, ‘lest you go …’. Pāṇini’s prohibitive construction is मा with the unaugmented aorist; मा with the future is a looser epic and Purāṇic usage.

  • उक्ता uktā

    Past participle of √वच्: ‘(you) having been told by me alone’ — that is, go now at a messenger’s word, while it is still a request.

Anvaya · prose order

सा त्वं मया एव उक्ता (सती) शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः पार्श्वं गच्छ; केशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवा (सती) मा गमिष्यसि।

sā tvaṃ mayā eva uktā (satī) śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ pārśvaṃ gaccha; keśākarṣaṇanirdhūtagauravā (satī) mā gamiṣyasi |

An imperative (गच्छ) and a prohibitive future (मा गमिष्यसि) set against each other: go now willingly, not later by force.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The messenger ends with a threat: let her go willingly at his word, or she will be taken by force, dragged by the hair. The threat sets up the next chapter, where Dhūmralocana is sent to do exactly that (6.3–4: केशाकर्षणविह्वलाम्).

Traditional interpretation

Dragging by the hair is the extreme insult to a woman’s honour in the epic world. The threat turns back on those who make it: the asuras sent to seize her are destroyed, beginning with Dhūmralocana in the next chapter.

Verse 5.128

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

एवमेतद् बली शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान्।किं करोमि प्रतिज्ञा मे यदनालोचिता पुरा॥⁠१२८⁠॥

evametad balī śumbho niśumbhaścātivīryavān |kiṃ karomi pratijñā me yadanālocitā purā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

एवम् · एतत् · बली · शुम्भः · निशुम्भः · च · अतिवीर्यवान्किम् · करोमि · प्रतिज्ञा · मे · यत् · अनालोचिता · पुरा

evam · etat · balī · śumbhaḥ · niśumbhaḥ · ca · ativīryavānkim · karomi · pratijñā · me · yat · anālocitā · purā

Word by word

  1. एवम्evamso, thusindeclinable (adverb of manner); predicate: ‘(it is) so’
  2. एतत्etatthispronoun एतद्, n. nom. sg.; subject
  3. बलीbalīstrong, mightym. nom. sg. of बलिन् (बल + possessive ini); predicate of शुम्भः
  4. शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject
  5. निशुम्भःniśumbhaḥNiśumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject

  8. किम्kimwhat?interrogative pronoun किम्, n. acc. sg.; object of करोमि
  9. करोमिkaromi(can) I do√कृ (tanādi 8U ‘to do’), laṭ (present), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  10. प्रतिज्ञाpratijñāvow, promisef. nom. sg. of प्रतिज्ञा; subject (verb ‘was’ understood)

  13. अनालोचिताanālocitāunconsidered, made without reflectionf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of प्रतिज्ञा

Translation

This is so: Śumbha is strong, and Niśumbha exceedingly valiant. What can I do, since my vow (was made) long ago without reflection?

In readable EnglishIt is just as you say: Śumbha is mighty, and Niśumbha of surpassing valour. But what can I do? My vow was made long ago, without thought.

Textual verification / uncertain: Textual variant (corrections.json, status ‘Textual Verification Required’): Gita Press निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान्, other witnesses निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः. The analysis follows the Gita Press text.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • एवमेतद् बली evametad balī → एवम् + एतत् + बली

    no change before a vowel; consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    The m of एवम् is joined to the vowel of एतत्; the final t of एतत् becomes d before the voiced b, written with a virāma and a space.

  • बली शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान् balī śumbho niśumbhaścātivīryavān → बली + शुम्भः + निशुम्भः + च + अतिवीर्यवान्

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    -aḥ before the voiced n becomes -o; visarga before c becomes ś; a + a → ā.

  • यदनालोचिता yadanālocitā → यत् + अनालोचिता

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before a vowel.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अतिवीर्यवान् ativīryavān

    prādi tatpuruṣa (अतिवीर्य) with the possessive taddhita -वत् (matup)

    Members
    अति + वीर्य (+ matup -वत्)
    Vigraha
    अतिशयितं वीर्यम् अतिवीर्यम्; तत् अस्य अस्ति इति अतिवीर्यवान् atiśayitaṃ vīryam ativīryam; tat asya asti iti ativīryavān
    Literally
    “possessing surpassing valour”
    In the verse
    exceedingly mighty

  • अनालोचिता anālocitā

    nañ tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अ (नञ्) + आलोचिता
    Vigraha
    न आलोचिता na ālocitā
    Literally
    “not considered”
    In the verse
    made rashly, without thought
Morphology & anvaya

  • करोमि karomi

    Present of √कृ, eighth class (कर-उ-मि → करोमि). The present in a deliberative question: ‘what am I to do?’.

  • अनालोचिता anālocitā

    Past participle of आ + √लोच् (tenth class, so -इत: आलोचित) negated with अन्- before the vowel. It echoes अल्पबुद्धित्वात् of 5.119.

  • बली balī

    Nominative singular of the in-stem बलिन् ‘having strength’: the n is lost and the i lengthened (बली).

Anvaya · prose order

एतत् एवम् (अस्ति): शुम्भः बली, निशुम्भः च अतिवीर्यवान्। किं करोमि? यत् मे प्रतिज्ञा पुरा अनालोचिता (कृता)।

etat evam (asti): śumbhaḥ balī, niśumbhaḥ ca ativīryavān | kiṃ karomi? yat me pratijñā purā anālocitā (kṛtā) |

A concession (एवम् एतत्) spelled out in two nominal sentences; a deliberative question; a causal clause (यत् …) giving the reason.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Devī concedes the messenger’s point about the brothers’ strength, as she did at first (5.118), and again pleads her old vow: she cannot set it aside. (A variant: for निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान् the GRETIL Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa and Freund read निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः, the phrase of 5.118; the Gita Press reading is kept, and the sense is the same.)

Traditional interpretation

The mock-helplessness (किं करोमि) and the claim that the vow was ill-considered (अनालोचिता) repeat the irony of 5.119. The vow cannot be broken, so the only way to her hand is through battle — which is the outcome she intends.

Verse 5.129

Śloka · anuṣṭubh
‹›

स त्वं गच्छ मयोक्तं ते यदेतत्सर्वमादृतः।तदाचक्ष्वासुरेन्द्राय स च युक्तं करोतु तत्॥⁠१२९⁠॥ॐ

sa tvaṃ gaccha mayoktaṃ te yadetatsarvamādṛtaḥ |tadācakṣvāsurendrāya sa ca yuktaṃ karotu tat ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

सः · त्वम् · गच्छ · मया · उक्तम् · ते · यत् · एतत् · सर्वम् · आदृतःतत् · आचक्ष्व · असुरेन्द्राय · सः · च · युक्तम् · करोतु · तत्

saḥ · tvam · gaccha · mayā · uktam · te · yat · etat · sarvam · ādṛtaḥtat · ācakṣva · asurendrāya · saḥ · ca · yuktam · karotu · tat

Word by word

  1. सःsaḥsuch (as you are), that onepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies त्वम्
  2. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of गच्छ and आचक्ष्व
  3. गच्छgacchago√गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  4. मयाmayāby mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of उक्तम्
  5. उक्तम्uktam(has been) saidpast passive participle (kta) of √वच्, n. nom. sg.
  6. तेteto yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, enclitic gen./dat. sg.; recipient with उक्तम्
  7. यत्yatwhat, whichrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. sg.; correlative तत् (the first of line 2)
  8. एतत्etatthispronoun एतद्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies यत्
  9. सर्वम्sarvamallpronominal adjective सर्व, n. nom. sg.; qualifies यत्
  10. आदृतःādṛtaḥattentively, with carepast participle (kta) of आ + √दृ (tudādi 6Ā ‘to heed, respect’), m. nom. sg., active in sense (‘having taken heed’); qualifies त्वम्
  11. तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. acc. sg.; correlative of यत्; object of आचक्ष्व
  12. आचक्ष्वācakṣvatell, reportआ + √चक्ष् (adādi 2Ā ‘to tell’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
  13. असुरेन्द्रायasurendrāyato the lord of the asurasm. dat. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); indirect object of आचक्ष्व
  14. सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of करोतु
  15. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  16. युक्तम्yuktamwhat is fittingpast passive participle (kta) of √युज् (‘yoked, fit’), n. acc. sg., used as a noun; qualifies the last तत्
  17. करोतुkarotulet (him) do√कृ (tanādi 8U), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  18. तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. acc. sg.; object of करोतु, with युक्तम् (‘that which is fitting’)

Translation

So go, you; all this that has been said to you by me, report that with care to the lord of the asuras; and let him do that which is fitting.

In readable EnglishGo, then, and tell the lord of the asuras faithfully all that I have said to you; and let him do whatever he thinks fit.

युक्तम् ‘what is fitting’ is rendered ‘whatever he thinks fit’, the usual sense of the formula.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • स त्वं sa tvaṃ → सः + त्वम्

    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)

    The visarga of सः is dropped before a consonant; the same in स च.

  • मयोक्तं mayoktaṃ → मया + उक्तम्

    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    ā + u → o.

  • यदेतत्सर्वमादृतः yadetatsarvamādṛtaḥ → यत् + एतत् + सर्वम् + आदृतः

    consonant sandhi (voicing); no change · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39); खरि च (8.4.55)

    t → d before the vowel of एतत्; the t of एतत् stays unvoiced before s; the m of सर्वम् is joined to the vowel of आदृतः.

  • तदाचक्ष्वासुरेन्द्राय tadācakṣvāsurendrāya → तत् + आचक्ष्व + असुरेन्द्राय

    consonant sandhi (voicing); vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha, guṇa) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    t → d before a vowel; a + a → ā (आचक्ष्वासुर-); inside the compound a + i → e (असुरेन्द्र).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • असुरेन्द्राय asurendrāya

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    असुर + इन्द्र
    Vigraha
    असुराणाम् इन्द्रः, तस्मै asurāṇām indraḥ, tasmai
    Literally
    “to the Indra (chief) of the asuras”
    In the verse
    to Śumbha, king of the asuras
Morphology & anvaya

  • आचक्ष्व ācakṣva

    Imperative ātmanepada of आ + √चक्ष्: चक्ष् + स्व, with the s lost between consonants (चक्ष्व). ‘Tell, report’.

  • आदृतः ādṛtaḥ

    Past participle of आ + √दृ; besides ‘honoured’ it means ‘attentive, careful’, an active sense. Here it describes the messenger: report it all faithfully.

  • करोतु karotu

    Third-person imperative of √कृ: ‘let him do’. The Devī leaves the decision — and so the war — to Śumbha.

Anvaya · prose order

सः त्वं गच्छ। मया ते यत् एतत् सर्वम् उक्तम्, तत् आदृतः (सन्) असुरेन्द्राय आचक्ष्व; सः च यत् युक्तं तत् करोतु।

saḥ tvaṃ gaccha | mayā te yat etat sarvam uktam, tat ādṛtaḥ (san) asurendrāya ācakṣva; saḥ ca yat yuktaṃ tat karotu |

Two imperatives addressed to the messenger (गच्छ, आचक्ष्व), the second with a relative pair (यत् … तत्) as its object; then a third-person imperative (करोतु) for Śumbha.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Devī dismisses the messenger: he is to report her words exactly to Śumbha, and Śumbha must decide. The chapter ends here; the next opens with the messenger’s report and Śumbha’s anger (6.2–3).

Traditional interpretation

The formula ‘let him do what is fitting’ courteously leaves the choice to the enemy, but the vow has already determined it: he can win her only in battle. In the Gita Press pāṭha the chapter’s last mantra is followed by the bīja ॐ, which belongs to the recitation, not to the Purāṇa, and the colophon names the chapter देव्या दूतसंवादः, ‘the Devī’s dialogue with the messenger’.

❁

इति श्रीमार्कण्डेयपुराणे सावर्णिके मन्वन्तरे देवीमाहात्म्ये देव्या दूतसंवादो नाम पञ्चमोऽध्यायः॥

iti śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe sāvarṇike manvantare devīmāhātmye devyā dūtasaṃvādo nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||

Reflecting on the chapter

Robbed of their offices by Śumbha and Niśumbha, the gods do the one thing left to them: they remember the Goddess’s promise and praise her on the Himālaya, finding her in every state of every being. Their hymn is answered at once, when Śivā steps out of Pārvatī’s body, is named Kauśikī, and leaves Pārvatī dark as Kālikā. The asuras learn of her only through her beauty, and the chapter ends in an exchange of words: an offer of marriage made from the pride of possession, and a vow of battle that the Devī calls foolish but cannot break (5.128–129).

Verses for study and memorisation

  • 5.6The boon recalled: remembered in calamity, she will destroy the gods’ gravest calamities at that very instant.
  • 5.9नमो देव्यै महादेव्यै — the opening of the gods’ hymn.
  • 5.14–16The first yā devī stanza: homage to the Goddess who in all beings is named Viṣṇumāyā.
  • 5.78–80The last stanza of the series: she who, as consciousness, pervades this whole world and abides in it.
  • 5.87Ambikā comes forth from the sheath of Pārvatī’s body and is celebrated in all the worlds as Kauśikī.
  • 5.116Grave yet smiling within, Durgā, by whom this world is upheld, answers Śumbha’s envoy.
  • 5.120The vow: whoever conquers her in battle, humbles her pride and is her equal in strength shall be her husband.
Vocabulary, constructions and insights

Principal vocabulary

  • विष्णुमायाviṣṇumāyāViṣṇu’s Māyā: the Goddess as the power named in all beings
  • आपद्āpadcalamity, misfortune (परमापदः, the gravest calamities)
  • स्मृताsmṛtāremembered: the condition on which she comes to help
  • संस्थिताsaṃsthitāabiding, present (in all beings)
  • चितिःcitiḥconsciousness, awareness
  • शक्तिःśaktiḥpower, capacity; the Goddess as power
  • भ्रान्तिःbhrāntiḥerror, delusion
  • अधिकारःadhikāraḥoffice, a god’s function in the cosmos
  • यज्ञभागःyajñabhāgaḥshare of the sacrifice, the gods’ portion
  • कौशिकीkauśikīshe who came from the sheath (कोश) of Pārvatī’s body
  • रत्नम्ratnamjewel; the best of any kind (elephant, horse, woman)
  • दूतःdūtaḥmessenger, envoy
  • प्रतिज्ञाpratijñāvow, solemn promise
  • भर्ताbhartāsupporter; husband

Important grammatical constructions

  • The dative of homage with नमः, and the -ai dative of feminine i-stems (प्रकृत्यै, कृत्यै, 5.9, 5.13).
  • The yā devī frame: relative या with correlative तस्यै, the instrumental of manner …रूपेण and the participle संस्थिता (5.20–76).
  • Perfects of √कृ in both voices for the same subjects (चक्राते, 5.3; चक्रतुः, 5.4).
  • Absolute constructions: genitive (स्तवादियुक्तानां देवानाम्, 5.84) and locative (तस्यां विनिर्गतायाम्, 5.88).
  • The passive imperative as a polite command (ज्ञायताम्, गृह्यताम्, 5.91; श्रूयताम्, 5.119).
  • The gerundive with the person told as subject (वक्तव्या, 5.103) and impersonal कार्यम् with the agent त्वया.
  • The general relative clause with present for future, closed by a future main verb (यो मां जयति … स मे भर्ता भविष्यति, 5.120).
  • Prohibition and warning with मा: मा … ब्रूहि (5.123), मा गमिष्यसि ‘lest you go’ (5.126).

Śākta insights

  • Remembrance is the gods’ only weapon, and it is enough: her promise was given for the moment of remembering (5.6), and the hymn ends by restating it as a fact about her (5.82).
  • No condition of a being is outside the Goddess: hunger, thirst and error are named beside intelligence, faith and peace (5.26–28, 5.35–37, 5.74–76).
  • All her forms are forms of one consciousness, which pervades the whole world (5.17–19, 5.78–80).
  • The Goddess who is praised and the Goddess who asks are one: Śivā answers from within Pārvatī (5.85–86).
  • Those who own everything cannot see her except as one more possession (5.100, 5.112); what they offer her is already hers (5.114, 5.116).
  • She is won by no one: her only ‘husband’ would be one who conquers her and is her equal in strength (5.120).

Śrīvidyā connections

The hymn shares many names with the Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa): Viṣṇumāyā (339, विष्णुमाया), Mahādevī (209, महादेवी), Śivā (53, शिवा), Durgā (190, दुर्गा), Kalyāṇī (324, कल्याणी), Śarvāṇī (124, शर्वाणी), Gaurī (635, गौरी), and several of the qualities of the yā devī stanzas, among them तुष्टिः (443), शान्तिः (447), कान्तिः (449), स्मृतिः (540) and लज्जा (740); the idea of the Goddess as consciousness is shared too (362, चितिः; 417, चेतनारूपा). In the narrative Pārvatī (246, पार्वती) and Ambikā (295, अम्बिका) are names of both texts. These are shared Śākta names and ideas, not evidence that one text depends on the other; the story of Kauśikī’s emergence belongs to the Devī Māhātmya, and no specifically Śrīvidyā doctrine is stated in this chapter.

Numbering
Gita Press counts every uvāca line and half-śloka as a mantra; the Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa (GRETIL, adhyāya 85) numbers by full ślokas and does not number uvāca lines, so its numbers differ.
Corrections to the digital text
5.99: वह्निरपि* ददौ → वह्निरपि ददौ
Variant readings (kept as printed)

Uncertain readings or interpretations
5.9, 5.11, 5.41-43, 5.59-61, 5.77, 5.82, 5.104, 5.128
Sandhi patterns
  • Visarga sandhi is by far the commonest junction: -aḥ before voiced sounds → -o (नमो देव्यै), visarga → s/ś before t/c (नमस्तस्यै, निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान्), and -aḥ + a → -o’ (दूतोऽहं, 5.106; शुम्भोऽत्र, 5.121).
  • सः and एषः drop the visarga before any consonant (स तत्र, 5.104; स मे, 5.120; एष महापद्मः, 5.96).
  • Savarṇa-dīrgha, with the Gita Press double avagraha marking a long ā (यथाऽऽपत्सु, 5.6; पुराऽऽसीत्, 5.97); yaṇ (संस्मरन्त्यपराजिताम्, 5.5; देव्युवाच); vṛddhi (तथैन्दवम्, 5.3; मैवं, 5.123); pūrvarūpa (स्तूयतेऽत्र, 5.85; तेऽङ्गणे, 5.95).
  • Final m → anusvāra before consonants throughout; t → n before a nasal (कस्मान्न, 5.100; वचनान्मम, 5.103); ch doubled after a short vowel (सर्वभूतेषुच्छाया-, 5.29–31).
  • One declared hiatus: आख्याता | अतीव at the pāda break of 5.90, where the reported speech begins.
Samāsa constructions
  • In the yā devī stanzas, karmadhārayas whose first member is the form itself (बुद्धिरूपेण, निद्रारूपेण …, ‘the form that is intelligence’).
  • Bahuvrīhis of epithet, including feminines in -ī from body-parts (चञ्चलापाङ्गी, 5.113; अतिचार्वङ्गी, 5.92) and nested ones (केशाकर्षणनिर्धूतगौरवा, 5.126).
  • Upapada tatpuruṣas with agent forms (सर्वकारिणी, 5.12; रत्नभुजः, 5.112; पुरन्दरः, 5.94), and dvandvas of the paired brothers (शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः).
Grammatical observations
  • स्म in 5.9 is printed short for the metre; read as the particle with the copula understood, or as स्मः with the visarga lost metri causa — the sense is the same.
  • प्रणताम् (5.11) is taken as a short genitive plural ‘of those who bow’; its derivation is uncertain.
  • The two voices of √कृ stand side by side for the same subjects (चक्राते, 5.3; चक्रतुः, 5.4).
  • In 5.104 the kept Gita Press reading makes सा देवी the subject of यत्रास्ते; the GRETIL and Freund reading is smoother.
  • Several epic or ārṣa forms are kept as printed: शृणुष्व (5.107), प्राप्स्यसे (5.114), parasmaipada व्यपोहति (5.120), मा with the imperative (5.123).
  • The closing prayer changes metre: vaṃśastha (5.81) and indravaṃśā (5.82) after the anuṣṭubh of the hymn.
Philosophical themes
  • Remembrance and the promise of help
  • The Goddess in all beings
  • Kauśikī and Kālikā: the emergence from Pārvatī
  • Possession and the vow
Open questions
  • 5.11: नैर्ऋत्यै (Gita Press) / नैरृत्यै (GRETIL, Freund) — orthographic only.
  • 5.104: सा देवी तां ततः प्राह (Gita Press) / तां च देवीं ततः प्राह (GRETIL, Freund) — Textual Verification Required.
  • 5.128: निशुम्भश्चातिवीर्यवान् (Gita Press) / निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः (GRETIL, Freund) — Textual Verification Required.
  • Interpretive doubts are flagged on 5.9, 5.11, 5.41–43, 5.59–61, 5.77 and 5.82.
